Others seen: a separate label for the held multiple rule, which is further from anything the sponsor stated; a separate label for a statement-based mark, to tell a capital account balance from a sponsor mark typed by hand; keep as built.
- **context:** The method is part of the lock text (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/lock.ts lines 59 to 69), so a Locked version keeps the code it was locked with; only a term's label can change afterwards. Terms live in core.taxonomy_term, domain valuation_method (sponsor_mark, market_multiple, dcf, cost, par_plus_accrued, weekly_report, report_indication). A new or renamed term is a migration; the mapping is code in @pb/calc with its Python twin. Tests that name the labels: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e/suggestions.spec.ts ('Locked as a sponsor mark') and the API test of a report-based suggestion locked as 'Indication from sponsor report' (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestion-commands.test.ts). Public standard a decider may check (not consulted here): the valuation technique names of the IPEV guidelines.

### VS-7: Are the people who approved or typed the figures behind an accepted suggestion refused both approvals of the valuation, or only named?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md 'Options considered' 6 (line 27), item 6 (line 108), 'After the verification' first bullet (line 120). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 1 (lines 11, 12 and 16). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 628 and 647.
- **builtDefault:** valuationMachine refuses dealTeamApprove and lock to anyone in sourceReviewers (notSourceReviewer, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts lines 14 to 25). ValuationWorkflowService.sourceReviewers (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts lines 805 to 828) reads, as the system actor, every suggestion whose valuation is a version of the same investment and quarter and takes the reviewers of the Accepted ones, so a Draft started by a reopen bars the same people. A suggestion's source reviewers are the approver of each extraction run an input came from, the earlier quarter's figures included, and whoever typed a figure (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/suggestions/derivation.service.ts lines 372 to 374; reviewersByKey in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/suggestions/sources.ts). A figure cited to the record (restated by an accounting import, or never extracted) names nobody: recordFigureOf returns no reviewers for it (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts lines 585 to 609), so whoever approved the import that supplied the figure is not barred. A source reviewer may still accept the suggestion (if in operations), prepare, send back and reopen. An earlier suggestion put on the same Draft and later replaced still bars its reviewers. There is no override.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: name the source reviewers and refuse nobody. Others seen: bar them from the final approval (lock) only, which is what SEC-5.6 asks about a preparer; bar only the reviewers of the suggestion whose keys the Draft carries now; also bar the approver of an accounting import that supplied a figure; allow an approval with a recorded reason when nobody else is able to approve; keep as built.
- **context:** SEC-5.6 (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md line 78) says the person who prepares a valuation cannot be its final approver; the bar on source reviewers goes further than that sentence. The owner's guidance of 2026-11-11 recorded in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md (lines 81 and 86): no safeguards beyond those the build guide names. On a walled position with few members the bar can leave nobody able to take an approval (the same record notes the seeded wall has no operations member). The table checks of mon.valuation hold the preparer rule only (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0004_mon.sql lines 106 to 108). The reviewers of extraction runs are the deal team and operations, the same people as the first approval step. Tests: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestion-commands.test.ts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e/suggestions.spec.ts; the preview's sourceReviewersOn in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/valuations.ts. Any change goes through the transition table of docs/18 section 1 (rule 14). Practice a decider may check: two-person approval of marks in limited partner valuation policies.

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 7 (line 109), 'Left out for now' (line 65), 'Four guards' (line 53). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md line 51. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 6, last sentence (line 57). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 614.

Nothing says a position-level statement is late or missing; the board's Missing marks card shows a suggested mark when one exists.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: one expected row per held co-investment and continuation vehicle position each quarter. Others seen: expect one only for a position that has sent one before, since not every sponsor issues a position-level statement; a setting by position or by sponsor fund in config; keep as built.
- **context:** doc.expected_document already allows a row keyed on (investment_id, period_end, doc_type) (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0011_documents.sql lines 89 to 105), so no schema change is needed. Status and age are derived when read from config documents.* (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/documents/expected.ts). A test pins that a position-level statement fills no fund row (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/statement-guards.test.ts). Whether every co-investment sponsor sends such a statement is a fact about the outside world the repository does not hold; expecting one from a sponsor who never sends it leaves a standing 'missing' row. Touched: expectedParentOf and its preview twin (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/documents.ts), the monitoring board (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/monitoring/board.ts), the access matrix counts of doc.expected_document (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts), docs/04 M3, docs/18 section 6, the synthetic expectations.

### VS-9: What on a real document tells the platform that a capital account statement is for one co-investment position and not for a fund commitment?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 8 (line 110) and 'Left out for now' (line 65: 'reading the company name from page one of a real statement'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 6 (lines 52 to 57). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 614.
- **builtDefault:** The kind of a statement is its link: linked to a position it is position-level, linked to none it is fund-level. The classifier's answer carries a document type, a confidence, evidence, a period end, our reference (INV-0000) and a sponsor fund name, and no company name (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/ai/src/schema.ts lines 226 to 237). The company name comes only from the file name convention '<period> <fund> - <type> - <company>.pdf' (parseDocumentFileName, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/ai/src/models/classifier.ts lines 97 to 112). statementHold (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/validation/src/documents.ts lines 89 to 121) sends a statement to a person when what it names does not come to exactly one position; a statement that names no position is treated as a fund statement and is never held. So a statement that names its position only by the sponsor's co-investment vehicle, or by the company on page one under a file name that does not follow the convention and without our reference, is classified as a fund statement at or above the threshold, fills the fund's expected row and raises no suggestion. Classified is terminal.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: the classifier also reads the company name from page one. Others seen: an alias table for sponsor co-investment vehicle names, as core.company_alias and core.fund_alias already hold other names; hold for a person every statement whose sponsor fund has both a commitment and positions unless it links to exactly one of them; read the account holder after 'Prepared for' and the vehicle named; keep as built.
- **context:** What real statements look like cannot enter the repository (CLAUDE.md rule 1, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/10_DATA_CLEARANCE.md); the rule has to behave correctly whichever way they name a position. SEC-5.3: a held document keeps the links it names and its evidence never names a company or a number. Linking is exact, never fuzzy, with match guards (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/validation/src/linking.ts). A change to what the classifier reads is a new prompt version (document_classifier, version '1', /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/ai/src/registry.ts), a change to classifierOutputSchema and to the prototype keywordClassifier, and an eval run (pnpm test:evals). Pages: the review queue line statementHoldLine (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/documents/documents.ts lines 144 to 166). The service: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/documents/documents.service.ts lines 439 to 588. Related: VS-10, since a wrong link cannot be put right after classification.

### VS-10: How is a classified document put on the right position, or on none, after it was linked wrongly?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 9 (line 111), 'Left out for now' (line 65), Consequences (line 73). RELINK_NOTE in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/suggestions.ts lines 521 to 522. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 614.
- **builtDefault:** Not built. documentClassificationMachine has Classified and Duplicate as terminal states and one human row, NeedsReview to Classified by the deal team or operations (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts lines 410 to 447). A classify command can set a link and cannot clear one: a link left out keeps what the classifier found (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/documents/documents.service.ts lines 683 to 852). For a statement on the wrong position the run page says: 'A classified document cannot be relinked yet. Dismiss the suggestion; a draft can still be started on the board.' (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/extraction/ExtractionRun.tsx lines 478 to 482). The approved run of the wrongly linked statement stays the statement on hand for that position and quarter (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/suggestions/sources.ts lines 295 to 335): the dismissed value is not raised again, but it stays beside any later report-based suggestion as a cross-check.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: a command that sends a classified document back to review, with a reason, refused once one of its runs is approved. Others seen: allow it after approval too by withdrawing the approved run (its suggestion superseded, any figure it promoted put right through a normal promotion), since the mistake is usually noticed through the suggestion, which exists only after approval; a reclassify that writes a new document row and leaves the old one marked replaced; keep as built.
- **context:** A new command is a new row in the transition table of docs/18 section 6, with rejection tests (rule 14). A document carries the wall of its position, so a relink moves it across walls (SEC-5.3): whoever relinks must be able to see both positions. Also touched: the expected row the document filled (doc.expected_document.received_document_id), duplicates that carry the original's links, doc.extraction_run.investment_id (copied from the document when the run is opened), the guard trigger of mon.valuation_suggestion that ties a suggestion to a run of its own position (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0023_valuation_suggestion.sql lines 126 to 137), and, for a report promoted to the wrong position, the lineage and figures on mon.quarterly_performance, a financial record that only another approved promotion changes (rule 5). Contracts: DocumentCommandBody. Pages: ExtractionRun.tsx, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/documents/DocumentDetail.tsx. Audit: every relink with before and after.

### VS-11: Is a mark suggested for a private credit position?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112: 'No suggestion for a private credit ... position'), 'Left out for now' (line 65), 'When none is raised' (line 47). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14 'None when' (line 124). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md line 183.

A credit position has mon.credit_terms and mon.credit_performance (par value, fair value, accrued interest) and no quarterly_performance; the generator writes no quarterly report or position-level statement for credit, and the tracker expects none (EQUITY_DEAL_TYPES, the one list shared with the tracker).
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: a statement-based suggestion by rule 12.1 for a credit position when a position-level statement arrives, with the report rules left off; a credit rule of its own (for example par plus accrued, a method the taxonomy already has, or the sponsor's reported fair value from a credit report) with its own docs/08 text and fixtures; keep as built.

The 'none when' cases each have an API test with their sentence (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestions.test.ts) and a page sentence (runOutcomeText in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/suggestions.ts). Whether credit sponsors send capital account statements for a single loan is a fact about the outside world. Any rule that gives a value is financial math: tests first (rule 12).

### VS-12: Is a mark suggested for a position not held in US dollars?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112), 'Left out for now' (line 65), Extraction paragraph (line 55). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14 'None when' (line 125: 'nothing is converted').
- **builtDefault:** suggestionBar answers not_usd when core.investment.currency is not USD (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts line 221). The statement prompt answers null for every money line of a statement not in US dollars (STATEMENT_TASK, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/ai/src/registry.ts). The valuation workflow writes every Draft in USD (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts line 273). There is no exchange rate table and no rate source in the repository. Every seeded position is in USD.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: a suggestion in the position's own currency, which needs a valuation that can carry a currency other than USD; conversion at a period-end rate from an approved rate table, which is a new table and a new data source, possibly a paid one; keep as built.
- **context:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/03_DATA_MODEL.md line 9: 'The prototype is USD only; mixed-currency math must fail loudly, not convert silently.' Rule 10 (never invent a number) covers a rate nobody approved. Money units are USD, USD_K, USD_M and USD_B only (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/units.ts). NAV sums, reports and the accounting layout assume USD, so this is a platform question larger than suggestions. CLAUDE.md section 4: ask before adding a paid connector or licence.

### VS-13: Who reads the keys a suggestion leaves on a valuation's inputs: every role that reads the valuation, or only the roles that read suggestions?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 11 (line 113). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 1 (line 16, 'They are part of the valuation: every role that reads the version reads them'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 651.
- **builtDefault:** GET /api/v1/valuations/{id} serves viewer, deal_team, operations, approver, investor_relations and auditor (READ_ROLES, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/routes-controls.ts lines 22 to 29). Its answer carries the stored inputs in every state and the lock text once Locked (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts lines 406 to 451). The inputs hold suggestion.id, .basis, .value, .periodEnd, .documentId, .page, .runId, .input.<key> for each input and .crossCheck.<basis> for each other source with a value. The version screen words them for every reader (suggestionFacts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/suggestions.ts lines 587 to 620) and shows the link 'Open the suggestion' only to the four roles (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/ValuationDetail.tsx lines 619 to 678). The suggestion itself is read by deal_team, operations, approver and auditor only, at the route and in row-level security.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: leave the suggestion.input. and suggestion.crossCheck. keys out of the answer for a role that does not read suggestions, with the lock text unchanged. Others seen: leave out every suggestion key but the id for those roles; also withhold the lock text from them and show the hash alone; keep as built.
- **context:** The lock text of a Locked version is the canonical text of the investment, period, version, method, inputs and value (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/lock.ts lines 59 to 69) and the page shows it beside the hash so a reader can check one against the other: leaving keys out of inputs while serving the lock text hides nothing, and withholding the lock text takes away that check. What the keys repeat for a viewer or investor relations inside the wall: enterprise value, net debt and EBITDA of the quarterly record, the statement lines in stg.extracted_value and the reference mark are already readable by those roles (access matrix, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts lines 82, 83 and 110); new to them are the suggested value and the other sources' values. No report or package reads a valuation's inputs (only workflow.service.ts reads the column). Narrowing a read is within least privilege; SEC-5.1 asks the page, the API and the database to agree, and mon.valuation.inputs is one column under one policy, so a narrower read is an API and page rule, not a database one. Touched: the valuationDetail schema in @pb/contracts, the preview route /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/valuations.ts, ValuationDetail.test.tsx, the API valuation tests.

### VS-14: Do history tables follow the wall of their base record, for every walled record and not only for valuation suggestions?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 12 (line 114: 'It changes a security policy on existing tables, so it waits for the owner's word'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 651. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md line 88 ('Not opened: history tables, the audit trail, the wire register, walls').
- **builtDefault:** audit.add_history (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0001_baseline.sql lines 113 to 132) creates <table>_history (row_id, op, actor_id, at, row_image as json) with forced row-level security and one policy, history_read: operations, approver and auditor by role alone (line 129). It is called for core.investment, core.commitment, core.lp_commitment, core.client, mon.quarterly_performance, mon.credit_terms, mon.credit_performance, mon.valuation, mon.cash_flow, mon.capital_notice, mon.realization_outlook, core.walled_record, core.wall_member, ops.feature_flag (migration 0006); core.company_alias (0010); mon.wire_instruction, mon.trade_ticket, deal.closing, deal.allocation, deal.legal_term (0013); rpt.package, rpt.qa_result (0019); mon.underwriting_case, mon.underwriting_case_point (0021); mon.valuation_suggestion (0023). The access matrix pins the result: operations, approvers and auditors read 2 images in mon.valuation_history and 4 in mon.valuation_suggestion_history, walled rows included, while they read only the open position's base rows; a wall member who is deal team reads no history (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts lines 85 and 162). No API route reads an image table (a search of /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src finds only deal.opportunity_stage_history, which is the pipeline's own record). audit.event is read by role alone in the same way, and its details, which hold suggested values and fair values, never leave the database through the API (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/audit/audit.service.ts line 28).
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: one migration that gives the history table of every walled record the visibility clause of its base table, with the access matrix to match. Others seen: wall mon.valuation_suggestion_history alone now and the rest later; keep role alone and rely on no route reading history; in the same migration put the client rule on the history of client-restricted tables (core.client, core.lp_commitment, core.commitment, deal.allocation, rpt.package, rpt.qa_result), which today are read by three roles that all read every client; treat the details of audit.event the same way.
- **context:** SEC-5.3 (walls, a must), SEC-5.1 (row-level security as its own layer), SEC-9.2 (history), SEC-11.5 (the evidence pack wants change history; an auditor outside a wall does not read the walled base rows today and would stop reading their history). The change only narrows who reads. A history row has no column for the wall key: the clause has to read it from row_image (investment_id for most; row_id for core.investment; through the parent for mon.trade_ticket, mon.underwriting_case_point and the deal tables keyed on an opportunity; core.walled_record and core.wall_member have no id and a null row_id). add_history is the one place the policy is written, so the procedure and the tables already created both change. CLAUDE.md section 4 lists 'changing security roles' among the things to ask before; a policy is not a role, and the record chose to wait. Tests: the *_history rows of READ_MATRIX and pnpm test:rls; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/classification.ts for the image tables. Migration numbers: 0023 is the last on disk.

### VS-15: When the extractor reads a share of the vehicle from a statement whose lines are in fact our own account, how does the reviewer say so, so that the statement still gives its value and the record stays true?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md 'After the verification', second bullet (line 121). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 629 and 646 ('not by a new reason code'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md rule 12.1 (line 211). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, 'The sources on hand' (line 139).
- **builtDefault:** shareLeftOpen is true when the run staged a share and nobody accepted or typed it: undecided, rejected, or an edit with no value (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts lines 502 to 507). Then statementOnHandOf puts no line on hand (lines 541 to 571), the statement gives no value with the reason no_ending_balance, and the approval of the run is refused (409) while a balance line is accepted: 'accept or edit the share, or reject the lines it scales' (openShareRefusal, lines 525 to 532; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/extraction/review.service.ts lines 518 to 524). A reviewer's decisions are accept, edit to a value, and reject (stg.extracted_value.decision allows accepted, edited, rejected); none says 'the document states no share'. So for a misread share on an own-account statement the reviewer either rejects the share and then the lines, and the statement gives nothing, or types the share as 1.0000, which rule 12.1 allows (above 0 and at most 1) and which gives the right value while recording a stated share of 100%, naming the reviewer as a source reviewer for a figure the document does not contain, and leaving out the line 'Read as our own account.' In the other direction, a wrong share that is accepted understates the value as B x p; only the contributions check and the variance badge show it, and neither blocks (VS-4).
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the fix as built. Seen: a review decision that a field the model answered is not stated in the document, made by a named person, after which the share is absent and the lines are our own account; a reason code of its own for a share nobody accepted, in place of no_ending_balance; keep as built and say on the review page that 100% is typed for an own-account statement; refuse a typed share of exactly 1 and require the decision above.
- **context:** Rule 10 stands: a share that was read and not accepted is missing, never 100%. A new review decision touches stg.extracted_value (its decision check constraint, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0011_documents.sql around line 171; a staging table, not one the accounting import reads), the review commands and their contract (extractionRunCommandBody), finalValuesOf and shareLeftOpen with their tests (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.test.ts lines 638 to 702), the preview twin (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/extraction.ts line 1273), the review pages (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/extraction/ExtractionRun.tsx, dialogs.tsx) and docs/18 section 7. If the reading rule changes, docs/08 rule 12.1 and fixture 29 change first. The prompt already says to answer our share only when the lines are the whole vehicle's and the statement gives it (STATEMENT_TASK); only the prototype extractor has been measured (VS-24).

### VS-16: Who may act on a suggestion: operations alone, or also the deal team or approvers?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112: 'Operations alone act') and 'Who does what' (line 49). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, the transition table (lines 111 to 118).
- **builtDefault:** accept, applyToDraft and dismiss are operations only in the machine (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts lines 813 to 850), at the route (POST /valuation-suggestions/{id}/commands, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/routes-controls.ts) and in the database (policy decide_operations, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0023_valuation_suggestion.sql lines 218 to 221). The deal team, approvers and auditors read. Viewers, investor relations and platform admins are given nothing, and the web sends no request for them.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: let the deal team dismiss with a reason; let the deal team accept; keep as built.
- **context:** Starting a Draft is operations' alone in the valuation workflow (docs/18 section 1, '(new) to Draft'; policy insert_ops on mon.valuation, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0006_history_rls.sql lines 160 to 161), so an accept by the deal team would widen who can start a valuation. A wider right to decide is a permission change (CLAUDE.md section 4: ask before changing security roles). Dismissal is remembered: the same basis, value and inputs are not raised again, so who may dismiss decides who can silence a source. 'Valuation suggestions' is already in the work lists of operations and the deal team (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/role_focus.json). Tests: machines.test.ts (forbidden transitions by role), the write cases of /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts, the page's role gates in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/Suggestions.test.tsx.

### VS-17: Does an approved accounting import only refresh the Open suggestions of a position, or may it also raise one?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112: 'An accounting import refreshes suggestions and raises none'), 'When it is derived' (line 45). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, 'What a derivation does' (line 131). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 602.
- **builtDefault:** promoteQuarterlyPerformance calls refreshPosition after it writes the quarter (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/intake/promote.ts line 240). A refresh derives each Open suggestion again and never creates one: derivationStep answers none with none_open when nothing is Open (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts lines 1116 to 1121). So an import that brings enterprise value and net debt for a new quarter, for a position with a Locked earlier mark and that quarter's figures on record, raises nothing although rule 12.2 would give a value; the next approved statement or report run for the position would. A suggestion row must name an approved extraction run and its document (extraction_run_id and source_document_id are NOT NULL, and the guard trigger checks the run, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0023_valuation_suggestion.sql lines 53 to 54 and 126 to 137).
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: let an approved import open a report-based suggestion with the intake file as its source, which means a suggestion that names an intake row in place of a run (a change to the new table and its guard, not to a table the accounting import reads); keep as built.
- **context:** The accounting file also carries the booked fair value for each position and period (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/accounting_layout.json; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0009-intake-framework-and-validation-library.md). The weekly report intake already turns approved weekly values into Draft valuations with the method weekly_report (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/intake/weekly/weekly.service.ts), a second path to a Draft that does not pass through suggestions. Whoever approves an import is not a source reviewer of a figure it supplies (VS-7). SEC-9.4 asks every input to name its source. Tests: 'the refresh after an import' in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestions.test.ts; the preview's intake route keeps the figures on record as they were, so it shows only part of this.

### VS-18: Does a cash flow approved after a suggestion was raised, or a position that closes, refresh the Open suggestion at once?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, 'When it runs' (line 141: 'A cash flow approved later derives nothing by itself: the next approval for the position, or accept, finds it'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md 'When it is derived' (line 45).
- **builtDefault:** The capital workflow writes the approved cash flow when a notice is reconciled (writeCashFlow, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/capital/capital.service.ts lines 715 to 760) and does not call the derivation. refreshPosition is called from three places only: the promotion of a quarter, a valuation lock and a valuation reopen (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/intake/promote.ts line 240; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts lines 600 and 617). So an Open report-based suggestion keeps showing a value after a flow that makes rule 12.2 not calculable, until another approval, an import, a lock, a reopen or an accept derives it again; accept then answers 409 and closes or replaces it. The contributions check of a statement-based suggestion is worked out at each read, so it shows the new record at once. A position that is exited or made inactive does not refresh its Open suggestions either.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: refresh the position's Open suggestions in the transaction that approves a cash flow and in the one that closes a position, as lock and reopen do; keep as built.
- **context:** A derivation takes an advisory lock on the position and quarter and can answer 409 'Another approval for this position and quarter is in progress' inside the command that caused it, so a funding command would gain that refusal. docs/18 section 3 (capital notice and funding) and section 14. Related: VS-5 (what a flow since the mark means for the report rules). Tests: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestions.test.ts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/suggestion-commands.test.ts ('the 409 at accept'); the preview hooks in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/suggestionDerivation.ts.

### VS-19: When a suggested value is put on a Draft already open for the quarter, does the Draft keep its own period end?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112: 'Using a value on a Draft keeps the Draft's date'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, the applyToDraft row (line 115).
- **builtDefault:** applyToDraft calls editDraft with the suggestion's method, value and keys (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/suggestions/suggestions.service.ts lines 322 to 337); editDraft never changes period_end (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts lines 342 to 394). A Draft dated a few days off the quarter end, inside priorYearPeriodEndToleranceDays, keeps that date while its key suggestion.periodEnd holds the calendar quarter end. The Draft's method and fair value are overwritten, earlier suggestion keys are replaced and its other inputs are kept. accept, by contrast, opens the Draft at the calendar quarter end.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the default. Seen: refuse applyToDraft when the Draft's date and the statement's period end differ; move the Draft to the statement's period end; keep as built.
- **context:** mon.valuation is unique on (investment_id, period_end, version) and version numbers follow the exact period end (nextVersion), so moving a Draft's date is not an edit the workflow has today (the PATCH body takes method, fairValue and inputs only). alignedQuarterEnd decides which quarter a date reports for (docs/08 section 5). The synthetic scenario period_end_shift exists so that a sponsor closing its books a few days off the quarter end is exercised (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/14_SYNTHETIC_DATA_SPEC.md line 82).

### VS-21: Are the tie tolerances by unit (US$ 2,500 in thousands, 25,000 in millions, 25,000,000 in billions) and the 2% contributions tolerance the right values, and are fixed values by unit the right form?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md item 10 (line 112). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/definitions.json valuationSuggestions.tieToleranceByUnit and contributionsTolerancePct (lines 90 and 91; the note calls them 'a draft set'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md rule 12.1, the two checks (lines 212 and 213).
- **builtDefault:** statementTieTolerance (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/suggestion.ts lines 269 to 285) takes rollForwardToleranceUsd (1.0) when every line was read in dollars, otherwise the configured figure for the coarsest unit a balance line was read in; a unit with no configured figure is refused. docs/08 gives the reasoning: five lines, each rounded to a whole thousand, or to two decimal places in millions and billions. A statement in millions printed to one decimal place can be up to 50,000 off per line and would fail the 25,000 test while correct. The contributions check compares the statement's contributions to date times our share with the approved contributions on the position and flags a difference above 2% of the record.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record beyond the values. Seen: work the tolerance out from the decimal places each line was printed with (half a unit of the last place per line) in place of a fixed figure by unit; other values; keep as built.
- **context:** Values are a config edit. A tolerance worked out from the printed figures is a change to the rule: docs/08 text, fixture /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/fixtures/29_statement_mark.json (worked examples 2 and 8), the Python cross-check and the staged value's text form all come first (rule 12). In practice a statement's contributions to date can differ from our record for reasons such as fees called outside the commitment, which bears on the 2%. Related: VS-4 (what a failed check does).

An event or a task about a walled position must not name it to anyone outside the wall (SEC-5.3), and a log or event carries codes, never figures (SEC-11.4). ops.task is readable by every authenticated user (read_all), and a reopen already writes tasks titled with the investment number and the period (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts lines 698 to 711).

### VS-28: Is nothing raised when a statement arrives for a quarter whose mark is already Locked, even when the statement's balance differs from that mark?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 14, 'None when' (line 127: 'a mark for that quarter or a later one is Locked'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0023-valuation-suggestions.md 'When none is raised' (line 47). Not among the twelve items; it is one of the rules of the set that the record and docs/08 line 202 call a first draft.
- **builtDefault:** suggestionBar answers mark_locked when lockedForOrAfter finds a Locked mark that reports for the suggestion's quarter or a later one (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts line 224; the rule has fixture 31). A statement approved after the quarter's mark is Locked therefore raises nothing and is shown only on its run page with that reason; an Open suggestion is closed (mark_locked) when the mark locks. A reopened mark frees the quarter again, since the refresh after a reopen runs in refresh mode and a later approval can then open a suggestion.
- **alternatives:** None named in the record. Seen: when a statement for a Locked quarter differs from the Locked mark by the variance flag or more, tell operations (a flagged note on the run page or the board, or a suggestion that can only lead to a reopen, which stays a person's command); keep as built.
- **context:** A Locked valuation never changes in place (SEC-9.3); only operations or an approver reopens it, with a reason, and that starts a new version (docs/18 section 1), so nothing here may reopen a mark by itself (rule 5). Sponsors often send a final statement after a first mark is locked, which is a fact about practice a decider may check. Touched if changed: suggestionBar and derivationStep with their tests, docs/18 section 14, fixture 31 if the reference rules move, the run page sentence in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/suggestions.ts (runOutcomeText), the board (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/Valuations.tsx).

## collect:analytics-fit-map

Theme: Value creation analytics, the deal fit measures and the co-investment map

### AN-1: Should the synthetic book be changed so that some marks trail company earnings and 'Mark and timing' is a smaller part of our gain, given that this moves seeded figures across the repository?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0014-value-creation-analytics.md, 'Open for the owner' line 48 and Consequences line 33 ('The generator was not changed; varying it would move golden figures across the repo and is the owner's call'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 9 'Owner items' line 313.
- **builtDefault:** The generator is unchanged. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts lines 676 to 734: ownershipShare = cost / (entryEv - entryNetDebt); every quarter's Locked mark is max(ev - netDebt, 10% of cost) x ownershipShare with method sponsor_mark; a cost mark sits at the entry quarter (line 552); a realized position's last quarter gets no mark (line 728). So a held position's mark is exactly our entry stake of the company's equity value, the three driver parts explain the whole unrealized change, and on the seed 'Mark and timing' is distributions and realized exits. Seeded result as a wall member (0014 line 42): gain 162,534,642.07, of which mark and timing 78,430,885.74. Nearly every mark sits above the EBITDA line on the scatter.
- **alternatives:** Record: leave the seed, or vary it (owner's call). Others seen: (a) vary every mark by a lag or a discount drawn from the main random stream (moves NAV, MOIC and IRR everywhere). (b) Draw the variation from a stream of its own, as places.ts did for head offices, so no other random draw moves, though every figure that reads those marks still moves. (c) Add named scenarios on a few positions in the scenario table of docs/14 (for example a mark held at cost while EBITDA grows, a mark one quarter behind), the way stale_valuation and the others are tagged, so most golden figures stand. (d) Vary only another profile or seed and leave seed 42 as it is.
- **context:** A data question, not a product rule; rule 1 (synthetic only) holds either way. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/14_SYNTHETIC_DATA_SPEC.md (determinism, seed 42 default, the scenario table, section 11 as the precedent for a separate stream). What reads the seeded marks: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/value-creation-oracle.ts, nav-oracle.ts and value-creation.test.ts (figures built by hand from seeded rows), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/generate.test.ts, the committed dataset under tools/synthetic/.synthetic, the recorded preview answers (build-preview, probe-preview 194 checks), the site's committed media under /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/site/media, the valuation suggestion and base case scenarios of docs/14 section 12 that scale a Locked mark. The session notes on decision 0022 (SESSION_LOG line 564) say a seed change 'moves the seed 42 datasets, the recordings and every test that reads those positions'.

### AN-2: What capital counts in our entry stake: all capital invested by the as-of date, follow-ons included, over the company's equity value at entry (built), or only what was paid at entry?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0014-value-creation-analytics.md 'Open for the owner' line 49; the rule itself is /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md section 8, 'Our gain by driver' (lines 100 to 107).
- **builtDefault:** entryStake and gainByDriver in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/bridge.ts (lines 124 to 132 and 167 to 194): stake = invested / (EV0 - net debt0); each driver part = invested x driver / entry equity value, held to ten places; other = gain less the three parts. The API passes the position summary's invested figure, which is every approved contribution dated on or before the as-of date (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/portfolio/metrics.ts line 102; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/analytics/value-creation.ts computePosition line 215, stake at line 302). A follow-on therefore raises the stake as if it had been paid at the entry equity value, and whatever that misstates lands in 'Mark and timing'. The pages say mark and timing holds 'follow-on investments' (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/analytics/ValueCreationTab.tsx lines 164 to 170; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/deal/ValueCreationCard.tsx line 86). The generator makes no follow-on contribution on an equity position, so the seed never shows the difference.
- **alternatives:** The record states the default and names no other. Seen: (a) as built. (b) Entry capital only: contributions dated on or before a cut (the entry date or the entry quarter end); follow-ons fall wholly into other. (c) Step the stake at each follow-on: a new slice at the company's equity value of the approved quarter the follow-on falls in, each slice bridged from its own start, with a reason and no value when that quarter's figures are missing. (d) A recorded ownership percentage on the position: no such column exists on core.investment, and that table is read by the accounting import, so a schema change there is not the build's to take. (e) Invested capital net of capital returned.

### AN-7: When do the deal fit measures stop being labelled 'Draft measures', and what would make the set final?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Status line 3 ('Accepted for the prototype, as a draft set of measures'), Consequences lines 47 and 50 ('a first draft for the owner to change'; 'the caps are the owner's to set'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 11 open items line 381; docs/08 line 117 ('a draft set'); docs/04 M5 line 89; docs/09 line 82; docs/11 row A2; the _note of dealFit in config/definitions.json line 48.
- **builtDefault:** The label is fixed text with nothing behind it: a plain badge 'Draft measures' in the card header and the note 'A draft set of measures: the rules and every threshold are configuration, and none of them is a recommendation' (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/pipeline/DealFit.tsx lines 94 to 97 and 114, pinned by DealFit.test.tsx lines 38 and 64). No flag, state, date or version records who set a threshold or when; the answer carries the thresholds and calcVersion only (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/fit.ts; contract from line 642 of /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/controls.ts). What is draft in substance: (1) every number under dealFit in config was the build's judgement on the owner's word 'use your best judgement', none traced to a firm limit or a named standard; (2) the synthetic book is concentrated, so several seeded deals score 0 on sponsor room and low on geography room; (3) the rules themselves (which positions, which basis, straight lines, the seven chosen) were set once and not reviewed; (4) there are no weights and no overall score. Decision records know three statuses only: Proposed, Accepted, Superseded (docs/decisions/README.md).
- **alternatives:** Record: the owner changes a threshold (a config edit) or a rule (docs/08, fixture 24 and the record). Seen: (a) The label stays until the owner says otherwise. (b) The label goes when each sub-question (AN-8 to AN-17) has a recorded decision with its reason and tests; the record's status loses 'as a draft set'; the sentence 'none of them is a recommendation' stays. (c) The label follows the data: the card and the API say whose thresholds are in use, 'illustrative' while they are the repository's own and a named, dated set once a deployment supplies its own (a version or basis key under dealFit). (d) The label follows a source: a threshold is final once the system reads it from a recorded limit (for example a vehicle's exposure targets when pacing_plan exists) and no person types it. (e) Split it: the rules final, the threshold values provisional.
- **context:** The real limits (concentration limits in fund documents and investment guidelines, sizing policy, how sponsors are ranked) are facts only the firm has and may not enter this repository (rule 1, docs/10); rule 9 puts them in config so they can be set at deployment without code, but no config/local override of definitions is wired yet. Rule 7 and one rule in one place: docs/03 lines 87 and 88 already plan mon.pacing_plan.exposure_targets (json) and track_record (M17, not built, no migration), which would hold the same limits dealFit.caps holds. docs/06 line 29 uses a badge for 'AI draft' that an approval removes; 'Draft measures' has no such step. The same 'first draft for the owner to change' wording sits on docs/08 sections 11 and 12 (decisions 0022 and 0023), so one rule for dropping the label could serve all three. A change of label touches DealFit.tsx, DealFit.test.tsx, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e/deal-fit.spec.ts, docs/04 line 89, docs/08 line 117, docs/09 line 82, docs/11 A2, decision 0018 and README line 17, the config _note and the preview recordings.

### AN-10: Is the fund relationship score right (100 for a commitment to the deal's own fund, 60 for another fund of the sponsor, 0 for none), and should it and the M5 fund-fit flag, which is not on the board yet, be one rule?

### AN-11: What should the concentration caps be (built: sector 25%, sponsor 20%, geography 70% of held NAV), and where should they come from?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Decision table lines 28 to 30 and Consequences line 50 ('the caps are the owner's to set'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 377 ('the caps are configuration'); config dealFit.caps line 57; docs/08 line 122.
- **builtDefault:** room in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/fit.ts lines 149 to 153: linearScore(share, cap, 0), so 100 with nothing held and 0 at the cap or over it; a cap at or below zero is refused. One cap per dimension for the whole book the caller sees. Geography is the four-term taxonomy (North America, Europe, Asia Pacific, Other; migration 0002 lines 56 to 59), hence the high 70%. On the seed one sponsor holds about two fifths of held NAV and one geography about three fifths, so deals with that sponsor score 0 on sponsor room.

(c) Read them from pacing_plan.exposure_targets when M17 exists, so each limit lives in one place. (d) A soft and a hard limit: full score up to the soft one, 0 at the hard one. (e) Derive a cap from the book itself (the largest current share plus a margin), which always shows room and so measures little.
- **context:** The true limits are firm facts (fund documents, investment guidelines) that may not enter the repository (rule 1, docs/10) and belong in config at deployment (rule 9). (b) needs the opportunity's allocation lines by vehicle (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/allocations.ts) and turns one score into one per vehicle. docs/03 line 87. API test 'refuses to answer without usable thresholds, and names the key' (pipeline-fit.test.ts line 228). fitThresholdsOf in apps/api/src/pipeline/fit.ts.

### AN-12: Is the basis of the three room measures right: the share of held NAV of the positions with a Locked mark, every deal type, as the Exposure tab has it, before the new deal and without unfunded commitments?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Decision line 36 ('shares of held NAV as the Exposure tab has them') and the table; docs/08 line 122. Part of the draft set; the record names no alternative.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/fit.ts lines 129 to 139: held = active positions as of the date; marked = those with a NAV; share = NAV of the marked positions in the bucket over NAV of all marked positions; no share when nothing is marked, when total NAV is zero, or when the deal has no sector or geography. Held positions with no Locked mark are left out of both sides. The deal's own target commitment is not added, so the score is the room before the deal, not after it.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep (it ties to the Exposure tab figure for figure). (b) Pro forma: (bucket NAV + target) / (total NAV + target), shown beside today's share, so the score says where the deal would leave the book. (c) Exposure as NAV plus unfunded commitment. (d) Cost, or share of commitments. (e) No score while any held position is unmarked, the stricter reading that the NAV total itself follows in docs/08 section 2.
- **context:** Rule 10 and docs/08 section 2 'NAV (a set of positions)' (never a partial sum) against the Exposure tab's practice of sharing out the marked positions (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/analytics/analytics.service.ts, exposures and navShareOf, lines 124 to 160 and 574 to 595). A target in another currency is never converted, so (b) has no value for it. Unfunded is defined per position in docs/08 section 2. M5's acceptance criterion 'those figures equal what the Exposure tab and the sponsor page report' (docs/04 line 96) and the API test at pipeline-fit.test.ts line 101 would need rewording for (b) or (c).

### AN-13: Is the deal size measure right: the target commitment over the median invested capital of held positions of the deal's type, full marks from 0.5x to 1.5x, zero at nothing and at 3x, and no score for a target in another currency?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Decision table line 31 and line 34; config dealFit.size line 58; docs/08 line 123. Provisional as part of the draft set.

Reasons: no_target_commitment (none recorded, or another currency) and no_comparable_position.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep. (b) Compare with closing commitments, not invested capital (a part-funded position reads small today); each position's closing holds its commitment. (c) Compare with the vehicle: the target against a share of the vehicle's size or its unfunded capital. (d) One-sided: only an oversized deal loses marks. (e) A configured target size per deal type instead of the median. (f) The book currency from config or from the vehicle, not a constant in code.
- **context:** Rule 9 (the 'USD' constant is a setting in code). No FX table exists, so conversion is not available from data on hand. Small samples (the median of one position is that position; fixture 24 covers the median of one to four values and of none). API test 'gives a measure no score when its figure is not recorded, and says why' (pipeline-fit.test.ts line 163). Wording in deal-fit.ts lines 112 to 121.

### AN-14: Should the seven measures stay side by side with no weights and no overall score, or should there be an overall fit figure or a ranking on the pipeline board?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Options considered, option 3 line 17 ('would also need weights nobody has chosen. Not built').
- **builtDefault:** No weight exists anywhere. dealFit returns seven independent scores in a fixed order (FIT_MEASURES, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/fit.ts lines 12 to 20). fitSummary (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/pipeline/deal-fit.ts lines 168 to 184) says how many have a score and names the strongest and the weakest. The radar gives each measure the same spoke. The pipeline board and list show no fit.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep none (the record's choice). (b) An unweighted mean of the scored measures with 'n of 7' beside it, and no figure when fewer than a set number are scored. (c) Configured weights (dealFit.weights) under the same rule. (d) No overall number but a count of measures under a configured floor ('2 of 7 below 25'), which the board can show and sort by. (e) Two readings: relationship (tier, track record, fund) and room (sector, sponsor, geography, size).
- **context:** Weights are the firm's judgement. An average over measures with missing inputs can read a gap as a view (rule 10). Tier, track record and fund relationship move together for an established sponsor, so equal weights count the relationship three times. A new rule needs docs/08, a fixture, the Python check and the contract first (rule 12). The card says no measure is a recommendation.

### AN-15: Should the fit be worked out over the portfolio the reader may see (built), so that two readers can get different scores for one deal, or over the whole book?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Consequences line 49, recorded as a consequence and not as an open item.
- **builtDefault:** PipelineService.fit runs loadOpportunityFit inside the caller's transaction, under row-level security and the walls (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/pipeline.service.ts lines 160 to 176). The card opens with 'Against the portfolio you can see'. The roles that read the pipeline read the fit: viewer, deal_team, operations, approver, investor_relations, auditor (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/pipeline.controller.ts lines 38 to 45 and 74 to 84). A walled deal is 404.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep. (b) Work it out as the system actor over the whole book and answer scores and shares only, as the allocation totals of decision 0013 are read; a reader could then back out the size of positions behind a wall, which widens what they learn. (c) Keep (a) and say on the card that readers with other entitlements may see other scores.
- **context:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md SEC-5.1 and SEC-5.3 (a wall must not reveal that a record exists) and least privilege (rule 3). API tests 'takes every figure from the book the caller may see' and the 404 test (pipeline-fit.test.ts lines 101 and 210). If a fit is ever stored for a decision (AN-16) the stored copy must say whose view it was.

### AN-16: Should the fit stay worked out on every read and never stored, or be kept as it stood when a decision was taken on the deal (for example at the IC decision)?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Consequences line 48 ('the fit is worked out on read from figures already held, and nothing is stored'), recorded as a consequence.
- **builtDefault:** No table. GET /api/v1/opportunities/{id}/fit takes an optional asOf and recomputes. Marks, flows and commitments are read as of the date, but the sponsor's tier, the deal's own fields and the thresholds are today's (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/fit.ts lines 141 to 147 read the current sponsor row; thresholds come from the running config), so a past date does not reproduce what a reader saw then. The page never passes a date (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/pipeline/api.ts lines 64 to 67). Each read is audited as opportunity.fit.read with no figures.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep. (b) Store the answer (scores, inputs, thresholds, calc version, as-of date, whose view) with the IC decision when it is recorded, written by the system in the same transaction and read-only afterwards. (c) Store nothing but make the as-of read faithful: tier and deal fields from the history tables, thresholds versioned by date. (d) Put the fit into generated IC material when the generators of M7 exist.
- **context:** (b) adds a column or a table in the pipeline schema (docs/03, a migration, classification under SEC-2.1, RLS matrix tests); it is not a table the accounting import or export reads. docs/18 section 4 (deal stages, the IC gate) and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/ic.ts. SEC-9.2 (history). The fit is not a core financial record, so rule 5's staging does not apply. A stored fit must follow the deal's wall.

### AN-17: Should the static preview work out the fit of a deal created in the preview, or keep answering that it has none?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0018-deal-fit-measures.md Decision line 43 ('It works nothing out: a deal created in the preview has no fit, and the card says so'); SESSION_LOG line 362.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/pipeline.ts lines 289 to 320: the recorded fit is replayed per deal and credential; a deal made in the preview, or an as-of date other than the recorded one, gets 501 not-implemented. DealFit.tsx then shows 'Portfolio fit: not worked out in this preview'.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep. (b) Work it out in the preview with dealFit from @pb/calc over the recorded portfolio answers (shares from the recorded analytics summary, the sponsor's pooled MOIC and commitments from the recorded sponsor page, the median from the recorded portfolio list), as the preview already derives valuation suggestions and base case comparisons.
- **context:** Decision 0008 (the preview engine). Preview tests in apps/web (pipeline.test.ts: the recorded fit replayed, the wall, 501 for a deal made in the preview) and probe-preview. The preview is the public demo on the site, so the empty card is what a visitor sees after making a new opportunity. Whatever the preview computes must be the API's rule, not a second one (rule 7).

### AN-18: How does a company's head office get onto the record in real use, given that no screen, route or import writes it and nothing geocodes an address?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0017-co-investment-map.md Consequences line 47 ('A real deployment needs head offices entered or imported for its companies; until then every position sits in the no head office recorded table. Nothing here geocodes an address').
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0018_company_hq.sql adds hq_city, hq_country (ISO 3166-1 alpha-2), hq_latitude and hq_longitude to core.portfolio_company, whole or not at all, under the table's policies (every signed-in user reads a company; the deal team, operations and the service write it) and its history trigger. Only the seed loader writes them, from /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/places.ts (50 real cities; about one company in ten gets none). No API route edits a company. A company created when a deal closes gets its name, sector and geography only (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/convert.ts lines 126 to 137). The map counts such positions on a tile and lists them under 'No head office recorded'; it never places them.
- **alternatives:** Record: 'entered or imported'. Seen: (a) A small form on the company (city, country, coordinates) for the deal team or operations, audited. (b) City and country typed or picked, and the coordinates looked up by the system from a public domain gazetteer shipped with the app (no request to another origin, no paid service); a city not in the list stays unplaced until coordinates are typed. (c) Captured on the opportunity and carried to the company at close (M5: data captured once). (d) Proposed by extraction from a sponsor report or CIM into a review step that a person accepts. (e) A column in an existing import; if that is the accounting layout, changing it is the owner's. (f) An outside geocoding service: a new external service and an egress entry (SEC-7.3), not the build's to take.
- **context:** core.portfolio_company is reference data, not one of the core financial tables of rule 5; history applies (the table has it). docs/03 line 44. Classified internal (SEC-2.1). Rule 10 (never placed by a guess: coordinates looked up for a typed city are a recorded fact about that city, a guessed city is not). docs/17 for a new write route (If-Match, idempotency, audit). The existing roles can already write the row, so no new role or permission is needed. Tests: packages/db guards (whole-or-nothing, range, country code), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e/map.spec.ts, the RLS matrix. The portfolio row carries hq whole or null (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/schemas.ts lines 147 to 173).

### AN-19: Should the map go on reading one page of 200 positions from the portfolio list and saying so when more exist, with its filters kept out of the address?

- **source:** Not listed as open in any record; a default seen in code. Decision 0017 line 21 chose that the map 'needs no endpoint of its own'; its verification line 59 names a 'partial page' test.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/analytics/map/MapTab.tsx: line 42 POSITIONS = { limit: 200 } (the list contract's maximum, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/schemas.ts line 129); line 169 partial = nextCursor is not null; lines 344 to 347 'Showing the first 200 positions; more exist', and every tile is marked partial. The page of 200 holds every deal type, so co-investments and single-asset CVs beyond it are missing even when fewer than 200 of them exist. The filters live in component state (line 120), where the value creation tabs keep theirs in the address.
- **alternatives:** (a) Keep. (b) Follow the cursor until the list ends. (c) Ask the list for the two deal types only, one request per type with the existing dealType filter. (d) A light read route of placed positions (the record set an endpoint of its own aside). (e) Keep the map's filters in the address like the other Analytics tabs.
- **context:** The seeded book is far below 200 positions, so no test or demo shows the gap. The page does say it is partial, so nothing is passed off as whole (rule 10). Tests: MapTab.test.tsx and map-model.test.ts in the same folder. investmentsQuery in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/app/queries.ts. The preview records the one request the map makes, so (b) and (c) change what is recorded.

### AN-20: When are the site's screenshots, recordings and brochures regenerated so that they show the co-investment map and the fit card?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0017-co-investment-map.md Consequences line 49 ('a media regeneration for a later session'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md line 81 ('the site's media does not show it yet'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 350 and 384.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/site/media (screens, videos, brochures) is committed because the host's build cannot run a browser; the scripts under /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/site/scripts (capture-screens.mjs, record-demos.mjs, make-brochures.mjs, narrate.py) make it from the preview. The screens folder holds value-creation, entry-metrics and deal-value-creation pictures but none of the map or of the fit card, and no script or page of apps/site names either (searched). The session notes say the recordings needed a video encoder the sandbox lacked; decision 0020 says narration needs an ffmpeg that can write Opus (line 27).
