- **context:** Not a product behaviour question. Decision 0015 (the site says only what is built; media is regenerated in a session and committed; each regeneration adds tens of megabytes to the repository's history, SESSION_LOG line 315). Decision 0020 (narrated recordings). The site's check (check-site, 75 checks). Another agent is building the Reporting, Packages pages now, so a media pass would also have to settle whether those pages are in it. If the 'Draft measures' label changes (AN-7), a fit card picture taken before then goes stale.

## collect:reporting-packages

Theme: Client reporting packages: the engine, the API and the pages

### RP-1: When a position held in one of the client's vehicles sits behind an information wall that the person generating the package is not on, should the package be built without it and say so, be refused, or be built by the system from the whole book?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 470 (Session 11 continued, 'Phase 4, the snapshot builder and the package API', Open items: 'A question for the owner, not yet decided ... Should the package say that positions were left out, refuse to generate, or be built by the system from the whole book?'). Spec section 12 item 1, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 108 ('Default: API unchanged; the page states the rule and never calls a package complete'). Decision record 0024 is not in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions yet (the last one there is 0023).
- **builtDefault:** The package is built from what the person who sends generate or regenerate can read, and it says nothing about what was left out. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/packages/packages.service.ts, private method generate: reads the client's commitments (core.lp_commitment) and then loadInvestmentsWithMetrics for those vehicles inside the caller's own transaction, so row-level security on core.investment (pb.can_see_walled, migrations 0006 and 0008) drops a walled position before the engine sees it. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/snapshot.ts (snapshotRequestOf, buildPackageSnapshot) makes lines and client lines only for the positions handed in. No rule in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/qa.ts looks for a missing position: QA-RDY-01 checks only the lines present; QA-TIE-01 walks only the current lines, so a position the last release carried and this one drops raises nothing; a package with no lines at all passes every check. So the client's share of NAV is short by the walled position with every check green, and the package may be labelled final. The static preview does the same (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.ts, positionsOf uses canOpen; test 'leaves out a position behind a wall the preparer is not on' in reportPackages.test.ts beside it). The sentence the spec plans for the Positions card ('Built from the positions {preparer} could read when it was generated. A position behind an information wall the preparer is not on is not included.') is not written yet: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/PackageDetail.tsx holds only the release gate card today (uncommitted, another agent is building it). Two facts the record does not state: any investor relations user entitled to the client may send generate or regenerate, so the lines follow whoever ran the last generation (the change log names that person) while prepared_by stays the person who started the package; and the API suite /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts has no wall case.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: (a) the package says positions were left out; (b) refuse to generate; (c) the system builds it from the whole book (all three in the session log); (d) the spec's default: API unchanged, a fixed sentence on the page, never the word complete. Others seen: (e) count, as the system actor, the positions held in the client's vehicles for the quarter and store on the package how many its lines leave out, the pattern already used for allocation lines (hiddenLines) and staged rows (hiddenRows); (f) a new QA rule for completeness that fails when the lines are not every position held, so the existing gate blocks release unless an approver overrides with a reason; (g) refuse or fail only a package labelled final; (h) under (c), store the whole content and filter lines, client lines and findings per reader with a count of what is not shown (this is RP-2); (i) extend QA-TIE-01 to flag a position the last released package carried that is absent now.
- **context:** Rules: CLAUDE.md rule 10 (never invent a number), rule 5; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md SEC-5.3 (walls restrict people; the system actor sees walled records to process the whole book and everything it produces is filtered per reader; nothing reveals a walled record exists), SEC-5.2, SEC-5.6, SEC-9.3 (the content hash covers the stored content), SEC-9.5. Docs: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md section 7 ('Which positions'; a new rule needs text there and tests first, see RP-13); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md M14 ('per-vehicle data readiness' is listed, the built rule is per position present); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 5; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/03_DATA_MODEL.md rpt.package row. Tables: rpt.package.content (jsonb: a key added inside it needs no migration, but the contract reportPackageContent in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/reporting.ts, stored packages and the hash change), rpt.qa_result, core.walled_record, core.wall_member, core.lp_commitment. Mechanism already in the service: asSystemActor from @pb/db runs the QA step inside the caller's transaction. Route: POST /api/v1/report-packages/{id}/commands. Tests to touch: apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts, apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.test.ts, packages/report-engine/src/*.test.ts, the RLS matrix in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts if a policy moves. Synthetic book: the walled_deal position sits in a vehicle the third client (ir.two) is committed to; the wall names deal.three and head.one only (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts near line 977), so ir.two's package for that client omits it today. Adjacent gap of the same kind: the Beach Primary Program holds fund commitments, not positions, so a client committed to it gets no line for it and nothing says so (the client look-through shows a placeholder with a reason for that gap: clientTotals in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/vehicles/vehicles.service.ts). Decide together with RP-2, RP-6 and RP-12.

### RP-2: Should a package's lines and findings be readable by every role that reads all clients, whatever walls those readers are outside, when the package carries a walled position?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 2, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 109 ('Latent: no investor relations user is on a wall. Default: no change; decide with question 1'), and 'Review points not taken' item 12, line 121 ('no API wall scenario is added now; it would pin behaviour the owner has not decided').
- **builtDefault:** A package is read by client, never by wall. Policy read_entitled on rpt.package in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0019_reporting_package.sql: the service role, pb.sees_all_clients() (operations, deal_team, approver, auditor since migration 0017), or pb.entitled_client(client_id). rpt.qa_result follows its package (pb.package_visible). GET /api/v1/report-packages/{id} (PACKAGE_READ_ROLES in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/routes-reporting.ts; loadDetail in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/packages/packages.service.ts) answers the stored content and every finding unfiltered: position numbers, begin and end NAV, flows, the client's share, expected and found. So when an investor relations user who is on a wall generates, the walled position's number and figures are stored in rpt.package.content and rpt.qa_result and are read by deal team, operations, approvers and auditors outside the wall, and by any other investor relations user entitled to the client. The company name is not in the content; the page adds a name only from the reader's own positions list (positionWords in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/packages.ts). The history tables rpt.package_history and rpt.qa_result_history are read by role alone (operations, approver, auditor: audit.add_history in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0001_baseline.sql). Audit events of a package carry counts only. Latent in the synthetic book: its one wall names no investor relations user. The preview mirrors it (test in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.test.ts: 'A reader on the wall reads the package as it was built: the line is not added for them').
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) no change, decide with RP-1 (the default). Others seen: (b) filter per reader on read: drop the lines, client lines, movers, footnotes, explanations and findings of positions the reader cannot open, and say how many are not shown; (c) keep walled positions out of every stored package (then RP-1 is always 'left out and said so'); (d) put the package itself behind the walls of the positions it carries, so only people on each wall read it, which narrows who reads; (e) state in docs/05 that a wall does not cover a position's figures once they are in a client's package, and change nothing. Note: if RP-1 is decided as 'built by the system from the whole book', this case stops being latent, since every package of a client in the walled position's vehicle would then carry it.
- **context:** Rules: SEC-5.3 (walls; searches and answers never reveal the record exists), SEC-5.1 (the same rule at the data layer: a filter only in the API leaves the row readable by a direct query, the lesson of migration 0009), SEC-5.2 (who reads every client is settled and is not this question; the same record says walls were not opened by it), SEC-9.3 (the hash covers the whole content, so a reader shown a filtered content cannot take the hash again), SEC-11.1 (reads of Restricted records). Tables and policies: rpt.package, rpt.qa_result, both history tables; decision 0023 question 12 (walling history tables) is the same matter for history and sits in another theme. Files: migration 0019 (policies read_entitled, read_visible); packages.service.ts loadDetail and rows; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/reporting.ts; preview route visibleTo in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.ts; the planned Checks, Positions and Client share cards. Tests: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts (72 cells for the two tables, none across a wall), apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts (no wall case), the preview test near lines 556 to 598.

### RP-3: Should a package have a way back once its checks have passed (from QA passed, In review or Approved) and a way to withdraw one that is stuck, given that an unreleased package blocks any new one for the same client, quarter and basis?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 3, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 110 ('Default: no new command; propose "send back with a reason" for docs/18'), and section 3 ('There is no way back from QA passed or In review. The page says so.'). The machine is one line in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 5 (lines 46 to 47).
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts, reportingPackageMachine: Draft to Generated (generate, investor relations); Generated to QAFailed or QAPassed (the system); QAFailed to Generated (regenerate) or to QAPassed (overrideQa, approver, reason); QAPassed to InReview (submit); InReview to Approved (approve, approver, not prepared_by); Approved to Released (release, investor relations or operations). Nothing else. So a QA passed package cannot be regenerated when the record changes after the checks; an approver in review can only approve; an Approved package can only be released. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/packages/packages.service.ts, create: 409 'already in progress' while the latest version for the client, quarter and basis is not Released. The only way to correct a package found wrong in review is therefore to approve it, release it, and start version 2 with a reason. Nothing withdraws or deletes a package (trigger no_delete and no DELETE grant in migration 0019; no such value in enum rpt.package_state), and no command changes the label, basis or as-of date set at the start: a Draft started by mistake, or one labelled final over a stale mark (QA-LBL-01 expects preliminary), keeps its slot until it is released, by override if need be. The page says it in NEXT_STEP_WORDS of /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/packages.ts ('There is no way back from here: a correction is a new version, started once this one is released.'), and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/StartPackageForm.tsx disables Start and links to the package in progress. The preview follows the same table.
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) no new command now; propose 'send back with a reason' as a docs/18 change (the default). Others seen: (b) build send back now: an approver returns an In review package to QA failed or Draft with a reason (the valuation machine has OpsPrepared to Draft with a reason, docs/18 section 1); (c) let investor relations regenerate from QA passed before submitting; (d) a return from Approved with a reason that clears the hash and alerts the approver (SEC-9.3 says any change reopens approval and alerts the approver); (e) a terminal Withdrawn state with a reason for a package started in error, which frees the slot (the underwritten base case has Draft to Withdrawn, docs/18 section 13): this adds a value to enum rpt.package_state, a migration on a table the accounting import and export do not read; (f) let the label be changed while the package is Draft or QA failed; (g) allow a new version to be started over an unreleased one, which then is marked superseded.
- **context:** Rules: CLAUDE.md rule 14 (every state change through the transition tables of docs/18; every forbidden pair needs a rejection test), rule 6; SEC-9.3, SEC-5.6, SEC-9.5; docs/04 M14 acceptance criteria. Files a new transition touches: docs/18 section 5; machines.ts and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts (generated forbidden pairs, pnpm test:workflows); reportPackageCommandBody in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/reporting.ts (a command is a member of the union; OpenAPI regenerated); packages.service.ts command and create; migration 0019 as it stands (Approved and Released require reviewer, time and hash; nothing requires them empty in earlier states; rpt.guard_released_package guards Released only; update policy move_package); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.ts and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/workflows.test.ts; web packages.ts (NEXT_STEP_WORDS, personCommands, gateSteps), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/lib/states.ts (packageTone), the work-link texts in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/role_focus.json. Tests that pin today's wording: packages.test.ts and PackageDetail.test.tsx ('says there is no way back once the checks have passed'). A change of state enum would also touch the classification and drift tests of packages/db.

### RP-4: May a package be started, pass the gate and be released on the net basis while the figures in it are gross, with only a notice on the page?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 4, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 111 ('Net packages show gross figures. Default: allowed, with the notice'), and section 7 ('Net packages carry: ...').
- **builtDefault:** The basis is a label and nothing more. rpt.package.basis is enum gross or net (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0019_reporting_package.sql); snapshotRequestOf and buildPackageSnapshot in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/snapshot.ts copy it into the content and compute the same lines whatever it is; QA-LBL-01 in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/qa.ts checks only that a basis is stated; the prior release for the tie-out is picked per basis (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/packages/packages.service.ts, generate). A gross and a net package of one client and quarter hold identical figures. The page shows NET_NOTICE ('Net figures are not worked out yet. These figures are gross of fees and carry.') above the release gate of a net package (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/packages.ts and PackageDetail.tsx). The notice is not in the stored content and not under the content hash, which records basis net. The record holds nothing to work net figures from: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/03_DATA_MODEL.md has no fee or carry terms for the firm's own vehicles, and docs/08 section 7 says fees and expenses paid outside the position are in neither flow total. The API suite starts net packages freely (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts).
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) allowed, with the notice (the default). Others seen: (b) refuse to start or to generate a net package until a net rule exists (the API answers 422 and the form offers Gross only); (c) keep the start and add a QA rule that fails a net package because net figures are not worked out, so it cannot move on without an approver's override and reason; (d) allow net only with the label preliminary; (e) write the notice into the content as a footnote code, so it is under the hash and reaches any later output file; (f) build net figures: needs fee and carry inputs on record, a rule in docs/08 with a golden fixture and the Python cross-check.
- **context:** Rules: CLAUDE.md rule 10 (a missing input gives a reason and no value; 'not calculable this quarter'), rule 12; SEC-9.5; docs/04 M14 ('preliminary vs. final and gross vs. net labeling', 'multiple reporting bases per client'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md row F7. What the synthetic clients' records list (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts near line 146): the first client gross and net, the second net only, the others gross, net and net_of_fees_and_carry, a third basis the package enum has no value for. Files: qa.ts (QA-LBL-01), snapshot.ts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/reporting.ts (reportPackageBasis), packages.service.ts create, StartPackageForm.tsx, packages.ts (NET_NOTICE, PACKAGE_BASES), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.ts, docs/08 section 7 table if a rule is added. Tied to RP-5 (which bases a client takes).

### RP-5: Should the API check a package's basis against the reporting bases on the client's record and refuse or flag one that is not listed, or is the page's warning enough?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 5, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 112 ('The API does not check the basis against the client's record. Default: the page warns, nothing blocks'), and section 3 (Start: 'one not on the client's record shows "Not on this client's record" and is still allowed').

### RP-6: Should a package show totals (each column over its positions, and the client's total share of NAV), and if so by what tested rule?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 6, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 113 ('No totals row. Default: none until a tested total exists in the report engine'), and section 5 ('The page does no arithmetic: it shows stored figures and has no totals row'). The first draft of the spec carried a totals rule that the revision removed; its text is in the scratch notes beside the spec, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/_result-3.txt, section 5.
- **builtDefault:** No total exists anywhere. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src has no totals function; the content shape reportPackageContent in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/reporting.ts has no totals key; the page helpers in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/packages.ts do no arithmetic by design. The Positions and Client share cards are not written yet. What the content holds: for each position, the whole position's begin NAV, contributions, distributions, gain or loss and end NAV (not the client's share), and in clientLines the client's share of end NAV only, one figure per position. So the client's total NAV for the quarter is not on the package, and there is no client share of the flows or of the gain.
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) none until a tested total exists (the default). The removed draft rule: a column's total is the unrounded sum over every line, begin NAV counting zero for a line entered in the quarter; if any other line has no figure in a column, that column has no total (not calculable, never a partial sum, never zero); functions packageTotals(lines) and clientLinesTotal(clientLines) in the engine. Others seen: (c) only the client's total NAV as the one headline figure; (d) totals stored in the content at generation, so the hash covers them and a later package can tie totals as well as lines; (e) a QA rule that the totals roll forward.
- **context:** Rules: CLAUDE.md rules 10 and 12 and SEC-9.6: a total is new financial math, so text in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md section 7, a golden fixture, property tests and the Python cross-check come before anything uses it (see RP-13 for what the engine has today). Rules already in the build for a total that is never partial: docs/08 section 2, 'NAV (a set of positions)' (a held position with no mark is never counted as 0), and clientTotals in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/vehicles/vehicles.service.ts (a share that is not calculable makes the total not calculable). The general guidance recorded in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md ('lead with totals and the headline figure, keep detail beneath'). An earlier item still listed as open for the client look-through, not for packages: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 92 ('Say if a labelled partial total is preferred'). Depends on RP-1: a total over lines that leave out a walled position, or the primary program's fund commitments, understates the client. Files: snapshot.ts, qa.ts, contracts reporting.ts (the content shape, so stored packages and the hash), packages.service.ts, the preview route, the planned Positions and Client share cards, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/06_BRAND_AND_UI.md section 3 for the format.

### RP-8: Which browser journey closes the Reporting, Packages pages and counts toward the Phase 4 exit: the override path the spec plans, or journey 5 of docs/12 (fail a check, fix the data, regenerate, release), with or without its layout step?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 8, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 115 ('Default: a packages journey; the Phase 4 exit stays unticked'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/12_TESTING_AND_QUALITY.md section 3 item 5 (line 28). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md Phase 4: the unticked pages line 48 and the Exit line 52 ('critical journey 5 passes; layout snapshot tests stable; QA catches every reporting scenario tag in docs/14'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 429 ('The exit of Phase 4 (journey 5, layout snapshots) needs the template engine').
- **builtDefault:** Nothing is built for it yet: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e has no report packages spec, and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic has no failingPackageCase (slice 8 of the spec). The plan is one journey over a client whose vehicle holds the roll_forward_break or the stale_valuation position: investor relations starts a final package and generates; the failed check shows expected and found and Submit is not offered; the approver overrides with a reason; investor relations submits; the approver approves and the hash shows; investor relations releases and the page is read only. Below the browser, the regenerate path is tested only for a tie-out explanation (API and preview suites) and for a mark locked in the preview session; 'identical layout' has nothing to test until the template engine exists.
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) a packages journey on the override path, the Phase 4 exit left unticked (the default). Others seen: (b) the data path of journey 5 now: fail, fix the record through the valuation workflow (prepare, deal team approval and lock by three different people), regenerate, pass, submit, approve, release, leaving only the layout step for the template engine; (c) both paths as two tests; (d) restate journey 5 in docs/12 as two parts (the gate now, the layout with the template engine), so the roadmap line for the pages is ticked against a defined test.
- **context:** Docs: docs/09 Phase 4 (lines 41 to 52); docs/12 sections 3 and 5 (e2e is required on pull requests that touch the web app or the report engine); docs/04 M14 acceptance criteria (identical shape geometry on regeneration; a failed check blocks release unless an approver overrides). Shared browser data (the spec's preamble): the specs share one API process and one dataset; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/e2e/journey3.spec.ts locks the only position with no mark for the latest quarter and suggestions.spec.ts reopens it, so a journey that fixes data by locking a mark must not depend on the order the specs run in and must leave the data as it found it. A Released package is immutable and nothing deletes one, so a second run in the same API process on the same client, quarter and basis meets a 409 or needs a correction reason. Which failing check data can fix: stale_valuation and final (lock a mark for the quarter), roll_forward_break (a Locked mark at the prior quarter end, if the valuation workflow lets a prior quarter be opened). The capability rows F3, F4, F6, F7 and F9 in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md still read 'N' although the QA engine, the record and the API exist. Files: apps/web/e2e, tools/synthetic (failingPackageCase and its generator test), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/scripts/probe-preview.mjs, docs/09, docs/12.

### RP-9: In the static preview, should a regenerated package read a cash flow booked earlier in the same session (a notice reconciled there), or only the recorded flows?

- **source:** Spec section 12 item 9, /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/9593e3d3-c421-5b4a-b2b1-7c7ce8f36ae0/scratchpad/specs/report-packages-web.md line 116 ('A flow reconciled in the preview session is not in a regenerated package (flows come from recordings). Default: accept'), and section 8 step 3.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.ts, positionsOf: marks come from versionsNow, so a mark locked or reopened in the session counts; flows come from the recorded one-pager's cashFlows only (or from the detail of a position created in the session). A flow written by a reconcile in the session sits on the simulated notice (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/capitalNotices.ts) and is not read. The preview's valuation suggestion derivation does read those session flows beside the recorded ones (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/suggestionDerivation.ts, near lines 217 to 222), so the preview holds two readings of the approved flows of a position. The API has no such gap: it reads mon.cash_flow.
- **alternatives:** In the record: (a) accept (the default). Others seen: (b) read the session's flows too, through one shared helper that the package route and the suggestion derivation both call; (c) accept and say it in the preview guide.
- **context:** Only the static preview is concerned: no table, policy, route or rule of the API changes. Docs: docs/08 section 7 ('Flows of the quarter'); decisions 0006 and 0008 (the preview and its shared read models). Rule: CLAUDE.md rule 7 (no two components doing the same job). Files: preview reportPackages.ts, suggestionDerivation.ts, capitalNotices.ts, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/overlay.ts and state.ts. Tests: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.test.ts (the group 'the record as the session leaves it'), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/scripts/probe-preview.mjs; the walkthroughs in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/components/PreviewGuide.tsx.

### RP-10: How are the two reporting scenarios the synthetic generator does not produce (qtd_ytd_basis and sign_flip) to be produced, and what proves the exit 'QA catches every reporting scenario tag', when the snapshot builder computes every line and so cannot itself show a gain as a loss or start a quarter from the year?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md Phase 4, unticked line 46 ('qtd_ytd_basis and sign_flip produced by the synthetic generator (they are listed in docs/14 and not produced yet; the engine's tests build them by hand)') and the Exit line 52. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 428 and 471 (open items). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/14_SYNTHETIC_DATA_SPEC.md section 3 (the tags).
- **builtDefault:** Neither tag occurs in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src. The two faults are proven only by snapshots built by hand in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/qa.test.ts (QA-QTD-01 and QA-SIGN-01) and snapshot.test.ts ('starts the quarter from the end of the prior quarter, never from the year'). Through the API a package's lines always come from buildPackageSnapshot (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/report-engine/src/snapshot.ts), which takes the begin NAV from the Locked mark at the prior quarter end, works the gain or loss out by formula, adds the stale footnote itself, ranks the movers itself and computes the client lines itself. So on a built package QA-SIGN-01, the wrong-basis branch of QA-QTD-01, QA-STALE-01, the dated-after-the-as-of branch of QA-ASOF-01, QA-MOV-01 and QA-CLI-01 cannot fail. What can fail from the record: a missing mark (QA-RDY-01, QA-QTD-01, QA-RF-01), a line with no dated figure (QA-ASOF-01), final over a stale mark (QA-LBL-01) and a tie-out break (QA-TIE-01). The API suite proves stale_valuation and roll_forward_break on the synthetic book and no other tag (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts, 'catches the reporting scenarios of the synthetic book').
- **alternatives:** In the record: have the generator produce both tags (the unticked line). Others seen: (a) the generator writes record data that would mislead a year-to-date computation (a position whose marks at the start of the year and at the prior quarter end differ, with flows in earlier quarters) and a position whose quarter is a loss, and the proof is that the built package starts from the quarter and carries the right sign; (b) the generator emits ready-made faulty snapshots as fixtures for the engine, since docs/14 words sign_flip as 'gain reported as loss in a draft'; (c) leave both to the engine's own tests and reword the exit criterion and docs/14 to say which tags are caught on the synthetic book and which by fixtures; (d) produce them when an input exists that can carry such a fault (the typed figures and report overrides of the template engine).
- **context:** Docs: docs/14 section 3; docs/08 section 7 table (which rule catches which tag: forward_entry_snapshot, qtd_ytd_basis, roll_forward_break, sign_flip, restatement, stale_valuation, two_clients_one_fund); docs/09 exit; docs/12 section 4 ('tests use only synthetic data from tools/synthetic'). Files: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts with its expectations and README, the report-engine tests, apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts and the preview test that mirrors it. A generator change moves the seeded dataset: byte-stable documents and every test that counts rows for seed 42 are affected unless the change draws no new random number, the way decision 0023 added its statements.

### RP-12: Is the two-person rule on a package enough as built, where only the person who started it is barred from approving, a different investor relations user may regenerate its content, and a person who holds both investor relations and approver may override the failed checks of a package they prepared?

- **source:** Not recorded as open anywhere in the repository or the spec; found while reading the code for this list. The spec says only (section 3): 'Approve is offered to every approver; the API refuses the preparer and the page shows that refusal.' Code: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts (reportingPackageMachine), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/packages/packages.service.ts (command, generate), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db/migrations/0019_reporting_package.sql.
- **builtDefault:** prepared_by is set once, at the start. Any investor relations user entitled to the client may send generate and regenerate (the machine names the role; the update policy move_package admits them), which replaces the content; the person of the run goes to the change log and prepared_by does not move. Approve is refused to prepared_by only (precondition notSameAs('preparedBy') in the machine, and the check reviewer_id <> prepared_by in the table). So a person who holds investor relations and approver, did not start the package, but ran the generation that produced its content, may approve it. overrideQa asks only for the approver role and a reason (requireReason): a person holding both roles may override every failed check of a package they started and generated, though they still cannot approve it; nothing in rpt.qa_result compares approver_id with the preparer. The same approver may override and then approve, and the override is one reason stamped on every failed finding of the run. The preview follows the API (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/preview/routes/reportPackages.test.ts, 'refuses the preparer as the reviewer, whatever roles they hold', has a second approver do the override). No synthetic user holds both roles.
- **alternatives:** (a) As built: SEC-5.6 names the final approver only. Others seen: (b) bar from approval everyone who ran a generation of the package (the people in its change log), as the underwritten base case bars 'the person who last changed the figures' (docs/18 section 13) and a valuation bars its source reviewers (docs/18 section 1); (c) bar the preparer and anyone who generated from overrideQa as well; (d) where checks were overridden, require the override and the approval to be by different approvers; (e) let only prepared_by regenerate. Each of (b) to (e) narrows who may act.
- **context:** Rules: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md SEC-5.6 ('the person who prepares a ... report package cannot be its final approver'), SEC-9.5 (an approver overrides with a reason), SEC-11.1; docs/04 M14 acceptance criteria; CLAUDE.md rule 14. Files: machines.ts (the preconditions of overrideQa and approve) and machines.test.ts; packages.service.ts command (the record handed to attempt holds preparedBy only); migration 0019 (a database rule would be a new migration on rpt.package or rpt.qa_result, neither read by the accounting import or export); the preview route; docs/18 section 5; the page's button words (personCommands in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/reporting/packages/packages.ts and the planned PackageActions.tsx). Tests: pnpm test:workflows, apps/api/src/test/report-packages.test.ts, the preview suite, the RLS matrix if a policy changes. Tied to RP-1: the positions 'the preparer could read' are in fact those of whoever ran the last generation.

- **alternatives:** (a) As built: the engine is treated as checks laid over calc functions that are themselves cross-checked. Others seen: (b) move the cases of fixtures 25 and 26 into JSON files and add a Python implementation of the snapshot builder and the QA rules that runs them, with the random cross-check; (c) cross-check only the snapshot arithmetic (lines and client lines) and leave the pass or fail rules to TypeScript; (d) require it only for rules added from now on (a total, a completeness rule, net figures); (e) store the calculation version with the content.
- **context:** Rules: CLAUDE.md rule 12 (every function in docs/08 gets golden-file and property tests plus the Python cross-check before anything uses it); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md SEC-9.6 (golden files, an independent implementation, the calculation version stored with results); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/12_TESTING_AND_QUALITY.md sections 1, 2 and 5 (the calc golden, property and Python cross-check gate; 100% branch coverage is asked of packages/calc and packages/validation, the report engine counts among the other packages). It bears on RP-1 (a completeness rule), RP-4 (net figures) and RP-6 (totals), each of which would add a rule to docs/08 section 7. Files: packages/report-engine/src/*.test.ts, packages/calc/fixtures, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/calc/src/cross-check, apps/worker-py/src/calc_check (fixtures.py, cross_check.py), the command pnpm test:calc. ## collect:platform

Theme: Platform, sign-in, hosting, CI and the way the build is run. Facts that changed since the task was written, read from GitHub on 2026-11-18 without changing anything: the repository has been public since about 15:33 UTC today; GitHub has assigned runners to the ci workflow's jobs again from that minute (run 76 on head 33f568c: the guard, security with CodeQL and dependency review, and ci all passed, e2e was still running when read); main has no branch protection and no ruleset; pull request 18 is open with 78 commits; the Cloudflare branch build check still fails on every push. Nothing was written inside /home/user/Portfolio-Beach and no file was written to the scratchpad. Nothing is decided below. Each item ends by saying which part, if any, only the owner can do.

### PL-1: Should the Microsoft Entra sign-in adapter (a real IdentityProvider that validates tokens, with MSAL wiring in the web app, switched off by default) be built now from decision 0019, or does all of 0019 keep waiting for the owner's answers and a 'go'?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0019-microsoft-365-integration.md: status line 3 (Proposed, nothing built), Proposal 1 (line 31), 'What can be built in the prototype' (lines 49 to 55). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md line 83 ('Not started: it waits on those answers'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 365 to 370 and 382.
- **builtDefault:** Nothing of 0019 is built. Sign-in is the mock only. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/adapters/src/identity/mock.ts: the credential is the mock user's external id. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/adapters/src/types.ts: IdentityProvider has one method, authenticate(credential). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/auth/identity.middleware.ts reads 'Authorization: Bearer' and never lets the service role sign in. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/auth/auth.controller.ts serves /api/v1/auth/me and /api/v1/auth/mock-users (404 in production or when the adapter is not a mock). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/config.ts: PB_MOCK_IDENTITY defaults to 'true'. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/adapters/src/registry.ts: assertProductionSafe refuses to start in production with any mock adapter or the mock identity flag. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/SignIn.tsx is a user picker; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/app/session.ts keeps the mock credential in session storage with an in-memory fallback. No token or MSAL library is in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/pnpm-lock.yaml.
- **alternatives:** (a) 0019's proposal: build the adapter and test it against tokens signed by a key made in the test with a made-up issuer; MSAL (authorization code flow with PKCE) behind configuration and off; the mock stays the default. (b) Build the API side only (token validation and role mapping) and leave MSAL until a tenant exists. (c) Build nothing until the firm's IT is involved, which is today's state. If built, still to settle: which library checks the signature (a new runtime dependency, or Node's own crypto), which claim carries roles, how Entra app roles map to the application roles in @pb/db (APP_ROLES), and where client entitlements (Principal.clientIds) come from, since 0019 does not say.
- **context:** Fixed rules: SEC-4.1 to SEC-4.5 and SEC-17.6 in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/05_SECURITY.md (Entra single sign-on only, tokens validated on the server on every request, no tokens in local storage, no mock in production); CLAUDE.md rules 2, 3 and 13 and section 3b (check current Microsoft documentation and record the version used). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/13_BUILD_APPROACH.md section 3 says identity is 'decided at merge'; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/02_ARCHITECTURE.md line 100 says app roles map to Portfolio Beach roles. 0019 says docs/02, docs/05 (a new section for connector and agent access), docs/07 and docs/13 change when it is accepted. Tests that would be touched: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/adapters/src/adapters.test.ts, the API's auth tests, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/SignIn.test.tsx, /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/app/session.test.ts. Owner-only part: none for the adapter and its tests, which need no tenant. Registering the two applications and proving a real sign-in belong to the firm's IT at merge; this repository must never connect to a tenant (CLAUDE.md section 3d).

### PL-2: Should a second, smaller API description for Power Apps, Power Automate and Copilot Studio (read endpoints only, in the OpenAPI version the connector tooling accepts) be generated from the contracts now, and which endpoints does it carry?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0019-microsoft-365-integration.md: Proposal 2 (line 33), line 52, Consequences (line 61), and Microsoft's limits at line 25. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md line 63 (Phase 6: 'Read API (OpenAPI), Excel add-in prototype').
- **builtDefault:** One document only. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/openapi.ts (buildOpenApi) writes OpenAPI 3.1.0 from the route table and the zod contracts; `pnpm openapi:generate` (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/openapi-cli.ts) writes /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/openapi.json, which is git-ignored. As generated in this working tree it has 95 paths (64 of them with a GET), a summary on every operation, one security scheme named bearer, and is 1,820,472 bytes. 0019 records that a Power Platform custom connector takes OpenAPI 2.0 only and a file under 1 MB, so today's document meets neither limit.
- **alternatives:** (a) 0019: generate a second, read-only document in version 2 from the same contracts, with tests for size, version, a summary on every operation and no write route. (b) Rely on the translation of a version 3 document at upload (0019 says Copilot Studio's REST tool does this, and that the tool is preview documentation); the size limit still fails. (c) A Model Context Protocol server as the route for agents (0019 read this only as search summaries). (d) Nothing until question 3 of 0019 is answered. Open inside (a): the list of endpoints (0019 names portfolio, positions, pipeline, analytics) and whether client-level reads are included.
- **context:** Rule 5 and SEC-9: if writes are ever added they go to staging endpoints only. SEC-5.2 and SEC-5.3: the caller is always a signed-in user, so walls and row-level security apply as for the web app. SEC-10.7 (least-privilege delegated scopes). Rule 8: the document is generated, never edited by hand. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/openapi.test.ts and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/17_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md section 3 are the existing conformance checks. Every read route is audited today, which a connector caller would inherit. Owner-only part: importing the connector into a real environment, and the licences (PL-5). Generating and testing the document needs no tenant.

### PL-8: What is done about Cloudflare's own build failing on every push to a branch other than main: is the cause found, are branch builds switched off, or is the failing check left as it is?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0015-public-site-and-hosting.md, amendment 'the first publish' (lines 59 to 63: 'the cause is not known yet', 'the choice is the owner's'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md line 451. Pull request 18, comments of 2026-11-12 20:49 and 21:12 UTC.
- **builtDefault:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/wrangler.toml at the repository root is what Cloudflare's connected build reads: name portfolio-beach, one routes line with custom_domain, [assets] directory apps/site/dist, [build] command `pnpm --filter @pb/site build:site-only`. It sets neither preview_urls nor workers_dev. Production builds from main publish. On the branch every push ends with the check 'Workers Builds: portfolio-beach' failed (latest on 33f568c, reported 15:39 UTC on 2026-11-18, about two minutes after the push, with no text on GitHub); Cloudflare's own comment on the pull request lists ten failed branch builds from 13 to 18 November. The earlier record also saw a second, immediate failure reading 'Preview creation failed: This Worker does not exist on your account'. Because of this check GitHub reports the pull request's mergeable state as unstable. No file in the repository controls whether Cloudflare builds branches.
- **alternatives:** The record's two: the owner pastes the log of a failed branch build so the cause can be found, or the owner switches off builds for branches other than main in the Worker's build settings. Others: try a repository-side setting and read the check's pass or fail on GitHub after the push (the log stays out of reach, the result does not): the pinned wrangler 4.161.0 config schema has preview_urls ('Whether we use <version>-<name>.<subdomain>.workers.dev to serve Preview URLs', schema default false) and workers_dev, and whether either is related is not established; or leave the check failing and state in docs/16 that it is not a gate and must never be made a required check.
- **context:** The repository's gate is guard, security, ci and e2e (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/.github/workflows/ci.yml); 0015 line 63 says the host's checks are not part of it. CLAUDE.md section 3b: do not rely on memory for a fast-moving service; the host's documentation site was outside the session's network when 0015 was written, so wrangler itself (help, config schema, source, dry run) was the evidence. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/site/README.md, 'Publishing' (lines 96 to 113). Branch previews are needed for nothing: the site publishes from main only. Owner-only part: reading the build log (it is only in the Cloudflare dashboard) and changing the build setting there. Not owner-only: a repository-side experiment, and writing down that the check is not a gate.

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0015-public-site-and-hosting.md: Context (line 9: the owner 'will change the domain at a later date'), Decision (line 24: originally 'No domain, account, zone or secret is in the repo'), amendment (line 51: the domain is carried in one routes line). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 296 and 310.

The site is live on that domain (0015 line 60).

(c) Hold the domain as a custom domain set on the Worker in the Cloudflare dashboard, so the file stays as 0015 first intended (whether a deploy without a routes line leaves a dashboard domain in place would have to be checked against wrangler, not assumed). (d) A new domain when the owner has one.

Config over code (rule 9) is already met: the domain is one line. Owner-only part: which domain to use is a fact only the owner has, and DNS and zone changes are acts in the owner's account. The build can make the site correct whatever the answer, since changing the line is the whole change.

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md line 9 (P0-3, unchecked: 'SHA pinning needs bash scripts/pin-actions.sh on a signed-in machine') and line 18. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 37, 65 and 97. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/16_GITHUB_AND_CLAUDE_CODE_CLOUD.md line 15. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/.github/workflows/ci.yml lines 2 and 3.
- **builtDefault:** Every action is referenced by tag with the comment '# pin to SHA': actions/checkout@v4, pnpm/action-setup@v4, actions/setup-node@v4, astral-sh/setup-uv@v5, github/codeql-action init and analyze @v3, actions/dependency-review-action@v4 in ci.yml, and actions/upload-artifact@v4 in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/.github/workflows/site.yml. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/scripts/pin-actions.sh resolves tags with `gh api` and has a --check mode that no job calls. Tried from this sandbox on 2026-11-18: `gh api` on another repository is refused, but `git ls-remote --tags https://github.com/actions/checkout` answers with the commit of each tag, so the tags can be resolved here without the GitHub CLI. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/.github/dependabot.yml already watches the github-actions ecosystem weekly.

- **context:** SEC-17.1, SEC-17.4 (gate G0), SEC-17.5 (the organization adds SSO, teams and protected environments), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/09_ROADMAP_AND_BACKLOG.md Phase 8. The repository being public (PL-15) and the Microsoft 365 questions (PL-4 to PL-6) both lead back to the same conversation with IT. Owner-only part: all of it. These are conversations and approvals only the owner can have, and facts about the employer that must not enter this repository.

### PL-30: Is the database engine decided as PostgreSQL, or does decision 0002 stay Proposed until the firm's IT says whether Azure SQL fits better?

- **builtDefault:** PostgreSQL only. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/db holds 23 plain SQL migrations with row-level security policies, triggers, history tables and security-definer functions; tests run on PGlite (Postgres in WASM, decision 0005); the Drizzle definitions mirror the SQL with a drift test. The API reads PB_DATABASE_URL or a PGlite folder (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/config.ts). 0002 asks that Postgres-only features stay inside packages/db so they could be re-implemented.
- **alternatives:** (a) Accept PostgreSQL for the prototype and keep 'revisit at merge with IT' as a note. (b) Keep it Proposed. (c) Start an engine-neutral layer now so a port is smaller. A port to Azure SQL would mean rewriting the access policies, triggers and history tables that the access matrix (1,775 tests) and the guards prove.
- **context:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/02_ARCHITECTURE.md guiding decision 2 ('PostgreSQL is the system of record'); SEC-5.1 (roles enforced in the API and again in row-level security); rule 7 (schema designed fresh from docs/03) and rule 8 (migrations as code). Owner-only part: which database estate the firm's IT runs and prefers is a fact only the firm has. Whether the prototype's own record says Accepted or Proposed is not owner-only.

### PL-31: Which workflow and job engine does the platform use (the stack names 'Temporal or Azure Durable Functions'), and is one needed in the prototype at all?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/13_BUILD_APPROACH.md stack table (line 27: 'Workflow and jobs: Temporal or Azure Durable Functions'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/02_ARCHITECTURE.md section 4 (apps/worker-ts: 'workflows, integrations, schedulers'). /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md lines 528 and 618 ('No outbox event is written: no API service writes ops.outbox and nothing relays it').
- **builtDefault:** No engine and no worker. /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows is pure typed transition tables with tests; every command runs inside the API request's own database transaction (for example the suggestion derivation behind an advisory lock). There is no apps/worker-ts folder, no scheduler and no queue; the quarter-end check and similar steps are API routes a person calls. CLAUDE.md section 5 mentions a Temporal dev server in docker compose for local machines, but no compose file is tracked.
- **alternatives:** (a) Decide the engine at merge with IT and keep the prototype in-process. (b) Pick one now and record it. (c) Add a small in-process job runner behind an adapter so scheduled work (expected documents, quarter-end checks, alert dispatch, idempotency purge) can be built and tested without either product. Durable Functions keeps everything in Azure; Temporal needs a hosted service or cluster.
- **context:** Rule 14 (every state change through the docs/18 transition tables) holds whichever engine runs them. docs/13 stack rows for messaging (Azure Service Bus) and hosting (Container Apps). Open items elsewhere that wait on a job runner: watchlist alert dispatch, the idempotency store's purge, the outbox relay. A hosted workflow service could be a new paid service, which is not the build's to add. Owner-only part: none for the prototype's in-process choice; what the firm's IT will host is its fact.

It was checked by measurement (each line starts within 0.07 seconds of its caption, none overlaps the next, loudness and peak) and by a speech recogniser that heard every caption word. (c) A paid voice service (0020 option 1): better voices, a cost per regeneration, and the scripts leave the machine. (d) A person records the lines (0020 option 2): a real voice in a repository of synthetic material, and not reproducible by script.

Tests: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/site/src/site.test.ts and `pnpm --filter @pb/site check`. Segregation of duties (SEC-5.6) means a person holding approver and operations roles is plausible in a small team, which is where precedence matters.
