## collect:everything-else

Theme: Everything else the repository records as open

### EE-1: When a reviewer approves an accounting or ops-drop file, should a validated row whose figures already equal the record be written to the record again, or only marked as dealt with?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 4 open items (line 125) and Session 5 close-out (line 173): 'Approval promotes unchanged rows too (the record then carries the file as its lineage); say if they should be marked promoted without a write.'
- **builtDefault:** Approve promotes every validated row the caller sees, unchanged ones included. apps/api/src/intake/intake.service.ts (command, promote) calls promoteQuarterlyPerformance in apps/api/src/intake/promote.ts for each target row. That write upserts mon.quarterly_performance with the approver, time, reason and source_locator intake_file:<id>/row:<n>, sets the document, page, run, prompt and model lineage to null, and writes one audit event quarterly_performance.promote per row. So an unchanged row replaces an earlier extraction lineage (document and page) with the file lineage, and the Performance tab loses its page citation for that quarter. The preview mirrors it on staged rows only (apps/web/src/preview/routes/intake.ts).
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: mark an unchanged row promoted with no production write. Others seen: write only the approval stamp and keep the earlier lineage; leave unchanged rows out of approve-all and show them as agreeing with the record; keep as built (the record then says the accounting system confirmed the quarter).
- **context:** Paths in builtDefault and context fields throughout this list are relative to /home/user/Portfolio-Beach. Rules: CLAUDE.md rules 5 and 6; SEC-9.1 and SEC-9.4 (lineage); docs/04 M2 AC (lineage on every promoted value, idempotent intake); docs/18 section 2; decision 0009 (promotion safety) and decision 0012 FIN-3 (an accounting restatement drops the extraction lineage). Tables: stg.intake_row (diff kind unchanged), mon.quarterly_performance (read by the accounting import and export, so no schema change is in the build's hands). Route: POST /api/v1/intake/files/{id}/commands. Pages: apps/web/src/pages/intake/IntakeFile.tsx, apps/web/src/pages/deal/PerformanceTab.tsx (source link per quarter). Tests: apps/api/src/test/intake.test.ts, apps/web/src/preview/routes/intake.test.ts, apps/web/e2e/intake.spec.ts. A promotion also triggers a fresh derivation of open valuation suggestions.

### EE-2: What can a reviewer do with a staged row that passed validation but that they believe is wrong, given the staging machine has no way from validated to rejected or back?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 4 (line 126), Session 5 close-out (line 171), Session 6 (line 203); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0009-intake-framework-and-validation-library.md ('Validated rows have no reject path in docs/18 either (owner item)').
- **builtDefault:** stagingMachine in packages/workflows/src/machines.ts (lines 95 to 126) has validated > approved only; reject runs from flagged only, by operations, with a reason. The command body offers approve or reject (packages/contracts/src/schemas.ts, intakeCommand). selectTargets in packages/validation/src/prepare.ts picks validated rows for approve and flagged rows for reject. A reviewer who disagrees with a validated figure can approve it or leave it, and a file with a row left alone never completes. The same holds for weekly values in stg.valuation (apps/api/src/intake/weekly/weekly.service.ts).
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: a sendBack-style command. Others seen: validated > rejected with a reason; validated > flagged by a person with a reason (a manual flag that then follows the flagged path); keep as built and let the file close when every other row is decided.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rule 14 and docs/18 section 2 (every transition in the table, every forbidden pair with a rejection test; packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts generates them, so a new row changes the generated set). SEC-9.1. Update policies on stg.intake_file, stg.intake_row and stg.valuation (migrations 0007, 0009, 0010) already admit operations and the deal team. Contract intakeCommandBody; routes POST /api/v1/intake/files/{id}/commands and the weekly commands route; pages apps/web/src/pages/intake/IntakeFile.tsx and apps/web/src/pages/weekly/WeeklyFile.tsx; preview routes apps/web/src/preview/routes/intake.ts and weekly.ts; file status rule fileStatusOf in packages/validation.

### EE-3: Rejecting a flagged row, and starting or applying a change request, are operations-only steps: what happens when the record sits behind an information wall that has no operations member?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 5 close-out (line 171), Session 6 (line 203), Session 8 (line 281); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0009-intake-framework-and-validation-library.md ('the owner decides whether walls must include one or the machine lets a deal-team wall member reject'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md ('The seeded wall has no operations member', 'the route dialog offers operations users only and the seeded wall has none').
- **builtDefault:** The staging machine gives reject to operations only, the change request machine gives start, apply and reject to operations only, the exception machine gives every command to operations only (packages/workflows/src/machines.ts). Row-level security hides a walled row from an operations user outside the wall (pb.intake_row_visible, migration 0009; change requests follow the investment wall, migration 0013). So a walled flagged row waits for ever unless an operations user is on the wall, and a walled change request can be routed only to an operations user inside the wall (pb.user_inside_wall, migration 0014). The seeded wall holds deal.three and head.one and no operations user (tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts lines 977 to 989); tests put ops.two on the wall to finish. The route dialog lists operations users only and says 'No operations user is available to route to.' (apps/web/src/pages/valuations/ChangeRequests.tsx line 224).
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: walls must include an operations member; or the machine lets a deal team wall member reject. Others seen: keep the machines and have the product say plainly that the item waits for an operations member of the wall (on the file, the queue and Data Health); add an operations member to the seeded wall only; have the system refuse to wall a record, or flag the wall, while it has no operations member.
- **context:** SEC-5.3 (walls), SEC-5.6; docs/18 sections 2, 8 and 9; least privilege (giving the deal team reject widens a role). Wall tables core.wall, core.wall_member, core.walled_record and their policies (migrations 0006, 0009, 0016); who makes walls (platform admin, per apps/web/src/pages/admin/roles.ts). Changing the seeded wall moves the seed 42 dataset, the preview recordings and the digest tests that hold the dataset byte for byte (tools/synthetic tests, apps/web/scripts/build-preview.mjs). Tests: packages/db RLS matrix (packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts), apps/api/src/test/intake.test.ts, change request tests, apps/web/e2e/intake.spec.ts.

### EE-4: The staging machine has a row 'flagged to validated after an edit', but no route serves it: should a flagged row be editable and revalidated in the product, or is the only fix a corrected source file?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md section 2 ('flagged > validated (after edit) | rejected'); seen in code, not listed in the session log: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/workflows/src/machines.ts (revalidate row, precondition 'a flagged row must be edited before revalidation') and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/packages/contracts/src/schemas.ts (intakeCommand is approve or reject only).
- **builtDefault:** No API route, contract command or page performs revalidate; nothing in apps/api/src calls it. A flagged row can be rejected by operations or left. A corrected value can arrive only in a new file with different bytes, because the same bytes are a duplicate by content hash (apps/api/src/intake/stage.ts). docs/14 section 6 says of the TYPE-03 defect 'a person confirms which quarter it reports for', yet no command lets a person confirm it.
- **alternatives:** Remove the row from the machine and docs/18 (the source file is always corrected and sent again). Build an edit command for operations with a reason, audited, keeping the file's cell beside the edit (the pattern of edited_value on stg.extracted_value, migration 0012), then run the rules again. Offer only a 'confirm as reported' step for findings that ask a person a question (TYPE-03), leaving figure errors to a new file.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 5, 6 and 14; SEC-9.1; decision 0009 (cells stay text as the file wrote them; a reviewer sees exactly what the file said); decision 0012 WF-1 (edits never overwrite what was read). Tables stg.intake_row (payload, findings, diff), stg.valuation (raw). Rules in packages/validation/src/rules. Pages IntakeFile.tsx, WeeklyFile.tsx. Any edit of a staged figure is still followed by a person's approval before promotion, so rule 5 holds either way.

### EE-5: A file whose staged comparison went stale (the record changed after staging) is refused at approval and cannot be staged again from the same bytes: should there be a 'stage again' step?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 5 close-out (line 172) and Session 6 (line 203); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0009-intake-framework-and-validation-library.md (open item in the promotion safety paragraph).
- **builtDefault:** promote in apps/api/src/intake/intake.service.ts reads the quarter on record again inside the approval transaction and refuses with 422 'the quarter on record changed since the file was staged; import the file again' when the diff differs (check in the promoteQuarterlyPerformance call). But intake is idempotent by content: the same bytes with the same source answer the existing file with duplicate true and stage nothing (apps/api/src/intake/stage.ts, receiveFile and contentHashOf). Only a file the layout refused (status new, no rows) is read again from the same bytes. So the refusal's advice cannot be followed without a fresh export whose bytes differ.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: a 'stage again' command. Others seen: take the diff again automatically at approval and show the reviewer the new diff to approve (no command, the row returns to review); let a duplicate import of a file with stale rows restage only those rows; keep as built and word the refusal to ask for a fresh export.
- **context:** docs/04 M2 AC (re-running an intake with the same file creates no duplicates); SEC-9.1; decision 0009. Tables stg.intake_file (content hash, status), stg.intake_row (diff snapshot, status). Routes POST /api/v1/intake/accounting/import and the commands route. Preview apps/web/src/preview/routes/intake.ts keeps the same rules. Tests apps/api/src/test/intake.test.ts (stale diff, duplicate, re-stage of a refused layout). The same shape applies to ops-drop files (apps/api/src/intake/ops-drop).

### EE-6: An approved weekly value becomes a Draft valuation written by the system actor on the reviewer's behalf, because the valuation machine has no creation row and only operations may create a Draft: is that the right way for a deal team approval to open a Draft?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 6 open items (line 204); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0010-phase1-intakes.md ('Who creates the Draft', in the weekly as-built section).
- **builtDefault:** WeeklyService.promote in apps/api/src/intake/weekly/weekly.service.ts applies validated > approved by the reviewer (operations or deal team) and approved > promoted by the service role, then inserts a Draft mon.valuation as the system actor, with the reviewer recorded as prepared_by, method valuation_method.weekly_report, inputs naming the file, row, report date and prior, and one audit event valuation.create_from_weekly. docs/18 section 1 lists '(new) > Draft, Operations, investment active', but valuationMachine (packages/workflows/src/machines.ts line 37) starts at Draft with no creation row, so the create is not checked by the table. A deal team reviewer is therefore named preparer of a Draft the table says only operations creates, and the preparer can then not approve it.
- **alternatives:** Add a creation row to the valuation machine (operations and service) and run the weekly promotion through it. Let only operations approve weekly rows (narrows a role). Record the system actor as preparer and the reviewer as a source reviewer (the pattern valuation suggestions use), so the deal team reviewer is barred from approving that mark without being its preparer. Keep as built.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 5 and 14; SEC-5.6 (preparer never approves); docs/18 sections 1 and 2; decision 0010; decision 0023 (suggestions open Drafts through the valuation workflow's own writes and name source reviewers). Insert policy on mon.valuation admits operations and service only. Routes: weekly commands, POST /api/v1/valuations. Tests: apps/api/src/test weekly suite, packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts, apps/web/e2e/weekly.spec.ts. A weekly Draft is never a mark: reports read Locked only.

### EE-7: For a weekly value, is the right comparison the latest Locked mark on or before the report date (or the weekly value promoted after it), and is one Draft per position and date the right limit?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 6 open items (line 204): 'the prior it is compared with is the latest Locked mark on or before the report date; a second Draft for the same position and date answers 409.'
- **builtDefault:** priorFor in apps/api/src/intake/weekly/weekly.service.ts takes the latest Locked mark on or before the report date (highest version that day), or the latest promoted weekly value reported after that mark and on or before this report date. weeklyVariance (packages/validation/src/rules/weekly.ts) is null with no prior or a prior of zero; a move at or beyond intake.weeklyVarianceThresholdPct (0.1) flags the row; a report date more than intake.weeklyStaleDays (10) before the as-of date is stale. A second approval for the same position and period answers 409 from the unique key of mon.valuation (versionConflict).
- **alternatives:** Compare with the last Locked mark only (never with an unapproved weekly value). Compare with the last Locked mark rolled forward for approved flows since. Let a second weekly value for the same date replace the open Draft's value (the applyToDraft pattern of valuation suggestions) in place of a refusal.
- **context:** docs/04 M2 weekly-report-intake (prior value, variance, threshold flag, reviewer decision); docs/08 section 2 (value change rule valueChange in packages/calc); config/definitions.json intake.weeklyVarianceThresholdPct and intake.weeklyStaleDays, and valuationVarianceFlag (the board's flag, a separate key with the same value 0.1); table stg.valuation (prior_source, variance_pct, flag); docs/14 section 8 (weekly samples built on this prior rule); pages apps/web/src/pages/weekly. EE-24 (how a stale mark is carried) bears on what 'prior' should mean.

### EE-8: In the look-through reconcile, is it right that the provider label 'Other' is left unmapped on purpose, that private credit positions are compared, and that the severity of each kind of difference is fixed in code?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 6 open items (line 205); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0010-phase1-intakes.md (look-through as built); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0009-intake-framework-and-validation-library.md ('Exceptions open at exception_severity.high (the taxonomy has no intake-specific term)').
- **builtDefault:** config/lookthrough_mapping.json maps eight sector labels and four geography labels and leaves 'Other' out, so a row labelled Other opens an exception (rule LT-MAP in packages/validation/src/rules/lookthrough.ts) instead of being put in a bucket. apps/api/src/intake/lookthrough/lookthrough.service.ts compares every active position of every deal type, credit included, within intake.lookthroughCostToleranceUsd (1.0) and intake.lookthroughNavTolerancePct (0.02). Severity is chosen in code: high for TIE and XREF, medium for MAP and DIFF; accounting, ops-drop and weekly exceptions all open at exception_severity.high. Nothing in the record changes from a provider file.
- **alternatives:** Map 'Other' to a taxonomy 'other' bucket so it stops raising an exception each run. Leave credit positions out of the reconcile or compare them on par in place of NAV. Move the severity per rule into config or into the rule table, or add an intake-specific taxonomy term. Keep as built.
- **context:** docs/04 M1 (outside sources are mapped to the taxonomy and never override it) and M2 lookthrough-reconcile; CLAUDE.md rule 9 (config over code) and rule 10 (never guess a bucket); taxonomy terms seeded in packages/db/migrations/0002_core.sql; ops.data_exception.severity; config note says the real provider vocabulary comes only after merge (an outside fact). Pages apps/web/src/pages/lookthrough and exceptions. Tests packages/validation lookthrough rules, apps/api/src/test/lookthrough.test.ts, apps/web/e2e/lookthrough.spec.ts (which restates the two tolerances, see EE-48).

### EE-9: Should the hand-written workbook and PDF readers and writers stay, or be replaced by published libraries as format needs grow?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 6 (line 207: 'say if a published library is preferred once the format needs grow'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0011-dependency-free-xlsx.md; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (option 2, packages/pdf).
- **builtDefault:** packages/xlsx (about 1,500 lines, 73 tests) reads and writes workbooks with every cell as text, deterministic bytes, hard limits and an injected inflater; packages/pdf (191 tests) writes text-and-lines PDFs and reads positioned text runs. Both run the same code in the API, the browser preview and the generator. Not supported by design: numbers as numbers, styles, dates as serials, merged cells, formulas evaluated, scanned pages, images, OCR.
- **alternatives:** Named in the records: a published spreadsheet library; published PDF writer, parser and renderer; the Python document worker with libraries approved by IT as the production parser (docs/13 names python-pptx, python-docx, openpyxl). Keep the hand-written packages for intake and use a library only for report output (the Phase 4 template engine has to write pptx, docx and xlsx with layout locks, which neither package does today).
- **context:** SEC-10.3 (upload safety limits), SEC-10.5 (dependencies), decision 0016 (dependency checks and licence deny list in tools/dependency-check); CLAUDE.md 'ask before adding a new paid connector or license' (a free library with a permitted licence is not that, a paid one is); lint rules that forbid the wall clock and randomness in deterministic packages; docs/09 Phase 4 template engine line; docs/02 line on report-engine ('TS orchestration, Python rendering'). A new dependency is recorded in a decision record.

### EE-10: A document indexed while the classifier is switched off stays Received and cannot be classified by hand: should a person be able to classify a Received document?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 open items (line 238); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (documents API as built: 'answers 409 until the table gains a Received > Classified row (open item)'). Note: the same record's wave 4 review says 'documents indexed with the classifier off land in NeedsReview' (line 223 of the session log), so the two statements disagree.
- **builtDefault:** documentClassificationMachine (packages/workflows/src/machines.ts lines 410 to 446) has Received > Classified (service, confidence above threshold), Received > NeedsReview (service), Received > Duplicate (service) and NeedsReview > Classified (deal team, operations). There is no row for a person from Received. Whether an item indexed with ai.classifier off now lands in NeedsReview (review fix WF-5, named in a comment at apps/web/src/preview/routes/documents.ts line 82) should be read in apps/api/src/documents/documents.service.ts before deciding; if it does, the open item is closed in code and only the record is stale.
- **alternatives:** Add Received > Classified for a person. Keep the table and always send an unclassified item to NeedsReview (the service flags it), so a person classifies from there. Related and owned by another reader: decision 0023 recommends a command that sends a Classified document back to review.
- **context:** docs/18 section 6; CLAUDE.md rule 14; docs/04 M3; kill switch ai.classifier (packages/db/migrations/0011_documents.sql, default on); routes POST /api/v1/documents/index and the classify command; pages apps/web/src/pages/documents (review queue, DocumentDetail.tsx classify form); tests apps/api/src/test documents suite, packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts.

### EE-11: Six extracted fields of a quarterly report have no home on the quarterly record (prior-year revenue and EBITDA, gross debt, capex, sponsor ownership, fund fair value and cost, headcount): should any become a column, or stay on the approved run?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 open items (line 239: 'Say if any should become a column'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (consequences: 'mon.quarterly_performance gains no column').
- **builtDefault:** config/extraction_schema.json, schema quarterly_financials: fifteen fields, of which revenue_ltm, ebitda_ltm, enterprise_value (ev), net_debt, cash and total_equity have a target column; period_end, revenue_ltm_prior_year, ebitda_ltm_prior_year, gross_debt, capex_ltm, sponsor_ownership_pct, fund_fair_value, fund_cost and headcount have none. Values without a target stay in stg.extracted_value on the approved run and are read through the document; nothing else reads them (valuation suggestions read the report's equity value from the record, not these).
- **alternatives:** Add columns to mon.quarterly_performance (a schema change to a table the accounting import and export depend on, which CLAUDE.md section 4 reserves for the owner). Put them in a new side table keyed by investment and period with its own staging and approval. Keep them on the run and expose a read of 'approved values of the latest run' where a page needs one (for example prior-year figures as a cross-check of the year-on-year rule, or fund fair value as a cross-check for suggestions).
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 5, 6, 7 and the ask-before list (tables the accounting import or export depends on); docs/03 (mon.quarterly_performance, stg.extracted_value); config/accounting_layout.json; docs/08 sections 4 to 6 and 12; SEC-2.1 classification of any new column (pnpm check:classification); promoteQuarterlyPerformance in apps/api/src/intake/promote.ts; packages/ai output schemas built from the config.

### EE-12: Drafted highlights and commentary on an extraction run carry no visible decision and cannot be edited: should the contract carry their state and allow a longer edit?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 240); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (extraction pages as built, and review fix AI-4: 'editing a narrative row needs a longer contract value than the 200 characters an edit carries and stays open').
- **builtDefault:** The run detail carries highlights as an array of strings and commentary as a nullable string, with no decision fields (packages/contracts/src/schemas.ts around lines 2018 to 2026). The edit command's value is plainText(200) (line 2099), too short for a commentary. 'Accept clean values' accepts the narrative rows with the rest; a narrative row with a style finding (EXT-STYLE-01, EXT-TEXT-01) can only be rejected. The page tells the reviewer that accept clean accepts the draft (apps/web/src/pages/extraction/ExtractionRun.tsx). Highlights and commentary reach mon.quarterly_performance only when their rows are accepted.
- **alternatives:** Add decision fields for the two narrative rows to the contract and show accept and reject state on the page. Add a narrative edit command with a longer limit and the house-style check run again on the edited text. Keep narrative as accept or reject only, and have a rejected narrative written by a person elsewhere.
- **context:** docs/07 (house style, commentary from approved numbers only), CLAUDE.md rules 15 to 17, SEC-8.4; config/style.json; packages/ai validate rules; table stg.extracted_value (rows 'highlights' and 'commentary'); route POST /api/v1/extraction/runs/{id}/commands; preview apps/web/src/preview/routes/extraction.ts; tests apps/api/src/test/extraction.test.ts, packages/contracts/src/documents.test.ts.

### EE-13: The extraction retry limits exist twice, in the state machine as fixed numbers and in config: which one rules?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 241: 'raising the config past them has no effect until the machine reads the record').
- **builtDefault:** retryBudget in packages/workflows/src/machines.ts (lines 452 to 461) refuses a retry after 1 schema failure or 3 transient failures, as literals. config/definitions.json extraction.schemaRetries (1) and extraction.transientRetries (3) are read by the runner (apps/api/src/extraction/runner.ts, settings in apps/api/src/extraction/settings.ts), and the smaller of the two applies. docs/18 section 7 states the same two numbers as part of the machine.
- **alternatives:** The machine reads the limits from the record it is handed (the runner passes the config values), so config alone rules. Remove the two config keys and keep the numbers as part of the machine and docs/18. Keep both and have startup refuse a config above the machine's ceiling.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rule 9 (config over code) and rule 14; docs/04 M4 AC ('schema-invalid output is retried once, then flagged', which fixes the schema number in the spec); docs/18 section 7; packages/workflows/src/machines.test.ts; preview apps/web/src/preview/routes/extraction.ts uses the same machine.

### EE-14: Which deal types report quarterly company figures is a code constant 'until the owner names its config key': should it be config, and under what key?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 242); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md; comment in /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/tools/synthetic/src/preview-book.ts (lines 172 to 181).
- **builtDefault:** EQUITY_DEAL_TYPES in packages/workflows/src/controls-suggestion.ts (lines 155 to 159: co_invest_equity, cv_single_asset, cv_multi_asset) is the one list in product code; apps/api/src/documents/expected.ts re-exports it as QUARTERLY_REPORTING_DEAL_TYPES for the expected-document tracker and the monitoring board, apps/api/src/analytics/value-creation.ts uses it for value creation, and valuation suggestions use it as SUGGESTION_DEAL_TYPES. tools/synthetic/src/preview-book.ts holds a second literal copy of the list, and apps/api/src/test/value-creation-oracle.ts a third for tests. Unrelated and unread: config/definitions.json has a key dealTypes that maps coInvest and cv to sample vehicle names.
- **alternatives:** A config key (for example a list under documents or a flag per deal type in the taxonomy) read by one strict reader. Keep it a constant, because the deal type enum itself is code and the tables that hold quarterly figures are chosen by deal type (decision 0004), and remove the copy in tools/synthetic by importing the one list. Derive it from the taxonomy term's own attribute.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 7 and 9; decision 0004 (the deal type decides which monitoring tables apply); docs/04 M3 tracker and M9 board; the preview book carries the list as data (apps/web/src/preview/book.ts, reportingDealTypes); tools/synthetic/src/verify.ts and underwriting.ts read the generator's copy. The unread config key dealTypes is covered in EE-30.

### EE-15: When a failed extraction run is retried by hand and the new run succeeds, its data exception stays open: should the system close it?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md, last paragraph ('Not closed here: resolving a Failed run's EXT-RUN-01 exception when its hand retry succeeds needs a service row on the exception machine ... left for a decision').
- **builtDefault:** A failed run opens ops.data_exception with source extraction and rule EXT-RUN-01 (apps/api/src/extraction/runner.ts). A hand retry of a Failed run queues a fresh run for the same document (apps/api/src/extraction/review.service.ts). exceptionMachine (packages/workflows/src/machines.ts lines 539 onward) lets operations alone acknowledge, resolve or close; there is no service row, so nothing resolves the exception when the fresh run succeeds and a person in operations must do it with a reason.
- **alternatives:** Add resolve for the service role with a fixed reason that names the succeeding run, limited to exceptions the system itself opened about a run. Keep operations as the only resolver and show on the exception that a later run of the same document succeeded. Link the fresh run to the old exception so the queue can filter it out without changing its state.
- **context:** docs/18 section 9 and section 7; CLAUDE.md rule 14; SEC-11.1 (every command audited); migration 0010 column grant on ops.data_exception (operations alone may update state, owner_id, external_ticket; a system write needs the policy to admit the service role, which is a policy change to review under docs/05); pages apps/web/src/pages/exceptions; tests apps/api/src/test/exceptions.test.ts and extraction.test.ts. The same question applies to exceptions about an unreadable document that is later replaced, and to look-through differences that a later run no longer finds (docs/18: 'a problem that comes back opens a new exception').

### EE-16: The documents service still writes a failed call's error message to the log, where the logging rule allows a name and a code only: is that a fault to fix now?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md, last paragraph ('documents.service.ts still logs a failed call's message').
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/documents/documents.service.ts lines 483 to 490 call logger.warn with { name: error.name, message: error.message } (or String(error)) when a classification call fails. The extraction runner was changed in the same review to log an error's name and a bounded code only.
- **alternatives:** Log the name and a bounded code as the runner does (one shared helper). Keep the message but pass it through the redaction of packages/ai/src/redact.ts. Leave as is for the prototype because the model is a local keyword classifier.
- **context:** SEC-8.9 (model, prompt version, tokens, latency and ids logged, never content), CLAUDE.md 3c (structured telemetry with no business content) and rule 4; docs/17 logging section; apps/api logging redaction; a model client's error message can quote the input it refused.

### EE-17: Which AI switches are on by default, and which model answers: extraction is off in the database, the classifier is on, the assistant is off, and every agent model is the mock?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 237); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/definitions.json agentModels._note ('Production model chosen and approved at merge').
- **builtDefault:** packages/db/migrations/0003_audit_ops.sql seeds ai.extraction false and ai.assistant false; migration 0011 seeds ai.classifier true. The API test harness and the Playwright API script turn ai.extraction on through the audited flags endpoint; a person running the app flips it under Admin, Flags. config/definitions.json agentModels is { default: mock, ops: mock }; nothing in product code reads that key (it is named only in page copy, apps/web/src/lib/assistant.ts). The models wired are the keyword classifier and the fixture extractor of packages/ai (apps/api/src/composition.ts), which answer from recorded truth and never from a model service.
- **alternatives:** Seed ai.extraction on in non-production seeds, since the prototype extractor calls no service. Keep it off as the safe default and say so on the extraction page (it does, but with the flag's key, see EE-39). Make agentModels the single place the composition reads to choose the client, or remove the key. A real model on synthetic PDFs needs an approved API key, which only the owner can provide.
- **context:** SEC-8.10 (kill switches), docs/17 section 5 and section 6 (mocks never reach production), CLAUDE.md 3b (real model only with synthetic data and an approved key), docs/16 section 3; docs/07; routes for flags (apps/api/src/flags); pages apps/web/src/pages/AdminFlags.tsx and extraction pages; evals pnpm test:evals. Whether the product behaves correctly whatever model is later chosen is already the design (adapter AiModelClient).

### EE-18: Decisions 0012 and 0013 list work left 'out of the prototype, recorded for the owner': which of these are real open decisions and which are simply later work?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (consequences) and /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md (consequences); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 244) and Session 8 (line 283); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/14_SYNTHETIC_DATA_SPEC.md section 4 ('Scanned documents and OCR are an owner item').
- **builtDefault:** Not built: OCR and scanned document variants (packages/pdf returns text layouts only); semantic search (full-text only, tsvector in doc.document); the Python document worker as production parser (apps/worker-py holds only the calculation cross-check); a real model on synthetic PDFs; CIM and primary fund report extraction; Teams approval cards (approvals are in-app); the monthly new-deal confirmation; AI-assisted legal term extraction (terms are typed); consent requests (M19); look-alike domain alerts; alert dispatch; a tasks route for the reopen alerts.
- **alternatives:** For each: build a prototype version on synthetic data behind an adapter with a mock (for example scanned variants with an OCR adapter mock, semantic search with an embedding adapter mock, the monthly confirmation as an in-app list with approve, legal term extraction with the fixture pattern); or leave it for after merge; or drop it. Teams cards and a real model need accounts and keys only the owner or IT hold.
- **context:** docs/04 M3 (semantic search), M4 (variants), M8 (monthly confirmation, Teams cards post-merge), M16, M19, M22 (AI-assisted extraction to review); docs/09 phases 4 to 6; docs/11 rows A1, B9, B10, C2, E1, E4, I9; CLAUDE.md 3c (new integrations ship with a kill switch, a health check and a probe) and the ask-before list (new paid connector or licence); packages/adapters (no notification or OCR or embedding interface exists in packages/adapters/src/types.ts).

### EE-19: Alerts are worked out but sent to nobody, and task rows are written that no page or route reads: how should a person learn of a watchlist flag, a due date, or a reopened valuation?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 7 (line 243: 'Watchlist alert dispatch (docs/11 E7) stays pending: the wave 2 brief left it out') and Session 8 (line 283: 'alert dispatch, a tasks route for the reopen alerts (the task rows exist)'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md rows C2, E7, H1.
- **builtDefault:** Watchlist flags are computed on read (evaluateFlags in apps/api/src/analytics/analytics.service.ts) and shown on the watchlist and Home; nothing is stored or sent. Capital notices carry alerts on the notice detail (due within capitalActivity.dueSoonBusinessDays, changed instruction, urgency language, unverified instruction; packages/workflows/src/controls-capital.ts); config capitalActivity.alertDaysBeforeDue [3, 1, 0] exists but no timer or job uses it to notify anyone. A valuation reopen inserts an ops.task per prior approver (apps/api/src/valuations/workflow.service.ts around line 700); no route in packages/contracts lists tasks and no page shows them. packages/adapters has no notification interface.
- **alternatives:** Decision 0012's plan: watchlist alerts as ops.task rows for the deal team. Build the task hub read (a route and a 'Your work' list on Home, which also gives the role work list its counts, EE-43) and write a task for each new flag, due-date step and reopen, with no outside channel. Add a notification adapter (mail or Teams) with a mock and a kill switch, dispatch from an outbox (EE-20). Keep flags as read-time only and make no tasks.
- **context:** docs/04 M9 ('alerts to the deal team'), M15 (task and notification hub, missed-items digest), M16 (alerts at T-3, T-1 and due date); docs/18 section 10 (durable timers in the workflow engine); docs/17 jobs; CLAUDE.md 3c; tables ops.task, ops.job, ops.outbox (packages/db/src/schema/ops.ts); RLS on ops.task; config/role_focus.json work lists; a stored alert needs a rule for when it is new (so a flag present every day does not raise a task every day) and for walls (a task about a walled position follows the wall).

### EE-20: docs/18 says every transition emits an outbox event, but no service writes one: should the outbox be written, or the sentence removed?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md base case entry (line 528: 'No outbox event is written: no API service writes ops.outbox and nothing relays it') and Session 12 (line 618: 'No outbox event is written, as for the other workflows'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/18_WORKFLOWS_AND_STATE_MACHINES.md opening paragraph ('write audit.event, and emit an outbox event').
- **builtDefault:** The table ops.outbox exists (packages/db/src/schema/ops.ts line 84, classified in packages/db/src/classification.ts) and nothing in apps/api/src inserts into it or relays it. Every command writes audit.event through the audit helper of DbService.run. Long flows (extraction batches) run inline in the request with a small promise pool; there is no workflow engine and no relay.
- **alternatives:** Write an outbox row in the same transaction as each transition and add a relay job that turns rows into tasks or notifications (the base for EE-19). Remove the sentence from docs/18 and drop the table until a consumer exists. Keep the table and state in docs/18 that the prototype audits and does not emit.
- **context:** docs/18 opening rule and section 10; docs/02 and docs/13 (Temporal or Durable Functions for workflows and jobs; none is used, see EE-49); docs/17 (jobs, idempotency); CLAUDE.md rule 7 (no two components for one job: audit event against outbox event) and rule 8; SEC-11.1. An outbox row must carry no business content beyond ids (docs/17 logging rules) and must respect walls when relayed.

### EE-21: docs/04 lists credit watchlist rules that are not built (coverage below the covenant level, PIK toggle or deferred interest, LTV above a threshold): should they be added, and with what thresholds?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md M9 ('Credit watchlist rules in config: coverage below covenant level, PIK toggle or deferred interest, LTV above threshold, maturity inside 12 months, payment status not current'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md rows E7 and E13 (partial). Seen in code; the record does not list the missing rules as open.
- **builtDefault:** evaluateFlags in apps/api/src/analytics/analytics.service.ts (lines 256 to about 430) raises: no_locked_valuation, stale_valuation, missing_prior_year, negative_ebitda, leverage_above_max, ebitda_decline, markdown, covenant_breach and covenant_waiver (from the reported covenant status), payment_not_current, past_maturity and maturity_within_12_months (the code name says 12 while the window is watchlist.maturityWithinMonths from config). There is no LTV rule, no PIK rule and no coverage-against-covenant-level rule, and config/definitions.json watchlist has no key for an LTV limit. Severity (bad or watch) per rule is fixed in code.
- **alternatives:** Add the three rules with thresholds in config (an LTV maximum; coverage compared with the covenant level held in mon.credit_terms; a flag when the PIK share of the coupon rises between two approved periods), tests first since they read docs/08 section 10 figures. Keep the reported covenant status as the only covenant signal and amend docs/04. Move severity per rule to config.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 9, 10 and 12; docs/08 section 10 (interest coverage, LTV, DSCR; null cases); tables mon.credit_terms, mon.credit_performance; docs/14 scenarios pik_toggle and covenant_breach (seeded and so able to prove the rules); config watchlist; pages apps/web/src/pages/portfolio/Watchlist.tsx (its threshold labels map, see EE-39) and Home attention list; tests apps/api analytics suite. docs/11 E13 also lists DSCR inputs and the maturity ladder view as pending.

### EE-22: A position nobody has set a realization outlook for answers 'none, 18 months' from a constant in code: is that default right, and should it be config?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0012-phase2-documents-extraction-monitoring.md (monitoring API as built: 'or the default { outlook: none, horizonMonths: 18, note: null, setBy: null, setAt: null } when nobody set one').
- **builtDefault:** DEFAULT_OUTLOOK in apps/api/src/monitoring/monitoring.service.ts line 55 is { outlook: 'none', horizonMonths: 18, note: null }. GET /api/v1/monitoring/outlook/{investmentId} answers it with setBy and setAt null when no row exists in mon.realization_outlook. The edit is deal team only, audited, behind realizationFlagEditableOnlyManually.
- **alternatives:** Answer 'not set' (no outlook and no horizon) so a page and a report can tell 'nobody looked' from 'no realization expected', in line with never inventing a value. Keep 'none' as the meaning of no row and move 18 to config (docs/04 says 'next 18 months'). Keep as built.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 9 and 10; docs/04 M9 ('Realization outlook (next 18 months, partial or full) changes only by explicit edit, audited'); docs/06 (missing-value placeholder); table mon.realization_outlook; contract schema for the outlook; pages Performance tab outlook card; reports that will print realization boxes (docs/04 M14 'realization boxes change only through Report Overrides'); preview recordings hold the default for every user, so a change moves recordings.

### EE-23: Is 'carry at cost until the sponsor's first mark' the valuation policy, and is a portfolio NAV for a quarter 'not calculable' whenever one held position has no Locked mark for that quarter or an earlier one?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 3 open items (line 91: 'Valuation policy to confirm'), repeated as still open in Sessions 5, 6 and 7; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0008-shared-read-models-and-preview-engine.md (the NAV series rule); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/14_SYNTHETIC_DATA_SPEC.md section 1.
- **builtDefault:** The generator books a Locked mark of method cost at the entry quarter end of a position whose first sponsor mark comes later (tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts around lines 544 to 545). The API has no carry-at-cost rule of its own: lockedNavSeries in apps/api/src/portfolio/metrics.ts (lines 329 to 362) counts each held position at its latest Locked mark for the quarter or an earlier one and answers null for the whole quarter when any held position has none. A position converted from a closed deal gets no mark at all (apps/api/src/pipeline/convert.ts writes no figure), so the first quarter after a real close makes the vehicle and portfolio NAV not calculable until operations lock a mark by hand.
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: no mark until the sponsor reports (then the generator and docs/14 change, not the API rule). Others seen: the product itself proposes a cost mark for a new position as a Draft valuation for the usual two approvals (system does the work, a person approves, rule 5 holds); show a NAV total with the unmarked positions listed and excluded, labelled as partial (see EE-25 for the same question on client totals); keep null.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 5 and 10; docs/08 section 2 (NAV series); IPEV Valuation Guidelines (price of a recent investment as a starting point for fair value) and the instruction's stated practice of fair value at the sponsor's reported NAV rolled forward for cash flows since; docs/18 section 1; valuation methods taxonomy (valuation_method.cost); components/NavTrendCard.tsx (line breaks at a null point); weekly report footnotes (config reporting.noValuationFootnote); tests in apps/api analytics and portfolio suites and the generator's digest tests. Closely tied to EE-24.

### EE-24: When a position has no Locked mark for the quarter, the portfolio pages carry the earlier mark unchanged while a reporting package rolls it forward for cash flows since: should there be one rule, and which?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md row E11 ('NAV roll-forward when sponsor marks are late, with automatic footnote: P ... the roll-forward itself is pending'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Phase 4 snapshot builder entry (line 461). The difference between the two rules is seen in code and not stated as open in the record.
- **builtDefault:** Portfolio grid, one-pager, analytics, watchlist and weekly report: positionMetrics in apps/api/src/portfolio/metrics.ts (lines 101 to 116) takes the latest Locked valuation on or before the as-of date as NAV, unadjusted, and puts that value at the as-of date as the terminal flow for IRR. A contribution or distribution after the mark date is counted in invested or distributed but not in NAV, so MOIC and IRR are off until the next mark. The weekly report footnotes the position (config reporting.staleValuationFootnote). Reporting package: buildPackageSnapshot in packages/report-engine/src/snapshot.ts (lines 210 to 214) takes the last mark plus contributions less distributions since it, and footnotes it as stale.
- **alternatives:** One shared function in packages/calc (last Locked mark rolled forward for approved flows since, flagged as rolled forward) used by every read, tests first with a golden fixture and the Python cross-check. Keep the unadjusted carry everywhere and change the package builder to match. Keep both and label each figure with its basis. Whichever is chosen, the figure stays a derived display value and never a stored mark.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 7, 10 and 12; docs/08 sections 2, 3 and 7 (fixture 26 covers the package roll-forward); the instruction's stated practice (fair value at the sponsor's reported net asset value rolled forward for cash flows since; ILPA reporting templates, IPEV guidelines); docs/04 M9, M12, M14, M19 (automatic stale-valuation footnotes); QA rule QA-STALE-01 in packages/report-engine/src/qa.ts; docs/14 scenario stale_valuation; valuation suggestions (docs/08 section 12) already reason about flows between marks (hasFlowBetween). Tests that pin current figures: apps/api portfolio, analytics and reports suites, preview recordings, golden fixtures.

### EE-25: A client's total is 'not calculable' whenever one of its vehicles has no pooled figures (today always true for a client in the primary program): should the client see a labelled partial total?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 3 open items (line 92: 'Say if a labelled partial total is preferred'), repeated as still open through Session 7.
- **builtDefault:** clientTotals in apps/api/src/vehicles/vehicles.service.ts (lines 92 to 115) answers invested, distributions, NAV and gross MOIC as null if any vehicle share is null; the commitment total is always answered. A share is null when the ownership percentage is not set or the vehicle has no pooled figures. The primary program holds commitments, not investments, so it pools nothing (lines 50 to 59) and every client in it shows the placeholder with the reason on the Clients pages (apps/web/src/components/ClientLookThrough.tsx).
- **alternatives:** Named in the record: a labelled partial total. Others seen: answer two things, the total of the vehicles that can be calculated and the list of vehicles left out with the reason, never one unlabelled number; give the primary program pooled figures from its commitments' called, distributed and reported NAV once a commitment carries a mark (the question another reader holds: how a fund commitment carries a mark); keep null.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rule 10; docs/04 M1 AC ('client look-through totals equal the vehicle totals for every vehicle whose ownership sums to 100%'), M17; docs/11 D8, G8 pending, I14; decision 0004 (look-through by lp_commitment.ownership_pct at read time); the owner's guidance of 2026-11-11 in decision 0013 ('lead with totals and the headline figure, keep detail beneath'); SEC-5.2 and SEC-5.4 (client entitlement); reporting packages build client lines from the same shares (packages/report-engine, QA-CLI-01); contract ClientSummary; tests apps/api vehicles and clients suites.

### EE-26: A reader who is shown fewer allocation lines than exist sees the placeholder for each vehicle's own total: should the API answer per-vehicle totals to every reader?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 9 owner items (line 314: 'the API could answer per-vehicle totals if wanted'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md last section (the reading taken: 'a vehicle's own total is the missing-value placeholder, not a sum of the lines shown').
- **builtDefault:** opportunityDetail.allocationTotals (lines, hidden lines, requested, received, missing received) is built from totals read as the system actor, so every reader gets the same deal-level totals and a count of hidden lines; tieOutFromTotals in packages/workflows/src/controls-closing.ts is the one rule. Per vehicle there is no system-read total, so the page shows the placeholder for a vehicle whose client lines the reader cannot all see (apps/web/src/pages/pipeline allocation components).
- **alternatives:** Add per-vehicle totals read as the system actor (a vehicle's total is not client data; the owner said commitment amounts are what matters and client amounts are secondary). Keep the placeholder. Show the vehicle total only when the reader sees every line of that vehicle (as built).
- **context:** SEC-5.2 and SEC-5.4; migration 0017 (pb.sees_all_clients includes the deal team; viewers and platform admins read no client data; investor relations its own clients); the owner's words of 2026-11-11 recorded in decision 0013; a single-client vehicle (client SMA) makes a vehicle total equal to one client's amount, so a per-vehicle total could reveal a client figure to a viewer: that case needs a rule; contract opportunityDetail; routes GET /api/v1/opportunities/{id} and the closing detail; tests apps/api pipeline and closing suites.

### EE-27: docs/04 and decision 0004 say a vehicle whose LP ownership does not sum to 100% after its final close 'shows a data exception', but the build only lists it on Data Health: should an exception row be opened?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md M1 features and M17 AC ('appears on the exceptions list'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0004-private-credit-and-lp-commitments.md consequences; /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 1 ('Review decision 0004'). The gap is seen in code and not listed as open.
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/data/data.controller.ts computes vehiclesWithOwnershipGap on each read of GET /api/v1/data-health: vehicles with a final close date whose ownership_pct sum differs from 1 by more than 0.000001 (a literal in the SQL). No ops.data_exception is ever opened for it (no exception source for ownership exists among accounting_import, ops_drop_import, weekly_intake, lookthrough_reconcile, extraction, documents and the change request check). The Data Health page shows the list only to roles that read every client (apps/web/src/pages/Data.tsx OwnershipCard).
- **alternatives:** Open one exception per vehicle as the system actor when an LP commitment changes or on a scheduled check, with the tolerance in config, closed by the system when the sum returns to 100% (ties to EE-15 on system resolution). Keep the read-time check and amend docs/04 and decision 0004. Refuse to set a final close date while ownership does not sum to 100%.
- **context:** docs/04 M1 and M17; docs/18 section 9 ((new) > open by the system actor); CLAUDE.md rules 9 and 10; SEC-5.2 (an exception that names a vehicle and a percentage is client-derived data: who may read it); tables core.lp_commitment, core.vehicle.final_close_date, ops.data_exception (readable by every signed-in user unless tied to a walled or restricted entity); docs/14 scenario lp_second_closing; exceptions queue page.

### EE-28: Should Data Health be readable by every signed-in role, viewers included, and should the API answer counts and an ownership check it cannot establish from the caller's partial view?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 1 open items (line 40: 'Decide whether Data Health should be visible to viewers (today every authenticated role can read the counts; the counts respect that role's visibility)').
- **builtDefault:** packages/contracts/src/routes.ts (lines 112 to 120) gives GET /api/v1/data-health roles [] with auth true, so any signed-in role reads it. apps/api/src/data/data.controller.ts runs every count under the caller's row-level security: orphans, open exceptions, stale positions, Locked valuations for the latest quarter, active positions. The ownership check sums the LP commitments the caller can see, so for a viewer (who sees none) the API answers every finally closed vehicle as a gap with a total of 0, and for investor relations a partial sum; the page hides that list for those roles, the API does not. The read is not audited.
- **alternatives:** Restrict the route to operations, deal team, approvers, auditors and platform admins (narrows access). Keep it open and have the API answer the ownership check only to roles that read every client, and null with a reason otherwise. Compute the health counts as the system actor so they read the same for everyone (counts reveal nothing of a hidden row, the pattern of countsByFile in apps/api/src/intake/stage.ts) and keep names out for roles that may not see them.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rule 10 (the API answers a 0 it did not establish); SEC-5.1 to SEC-5.4; docs/04 M1 (Data Health page); least privilege; config/role_focus.json lists Data health in the auditor's work list; pages apps/web/src/pages/Data.tsx and Home (Data Health section); contract s.dataHealth; preview recordings for all eleven users; tests apps/api/src/test/api.test.ts. Ties to EE-27 and EE-29.

### EE-29: The Data Health endpoint answers 500 for an impossible date, silently reads a malformed date as today, and falls back to 75 days when config is missing: should it be brought in line with every other endpoint?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 3 open items (line 95). The log says the file could not be read in sessions because the repository's deny rule Read(./data/**) in .claude/settings.json also matches apps/api/src/data/.
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/data/data.controller.ts today: asOf is accepted when it matches four digits, two digits, two digits and otherwise becomes the clock's today (lines 29 to 32), so asOf=2025-02-30 reaches the SQL cast and fails with 500 and asOf=garbage is ignored; staleAfterDays is this.definitions.watchlist?.missingFinancialsDays ?? 75 (line 33), a default in code; the ownership tolerance 0.000001 is a literal. The fix named in the log is parseOrProblem for asOf and configInteger for watchlist.missingFinancialsDays (the shared helpers in apps/api/src/common).
- **alternatives:** Apply the named fix (400 for an impossible or malformed date, 500 configuration problem naming the key when config is missing). Also narrow the deny rule to the root data folder (a change to .claude/settings.json, which is the owner's configuration and not the build's to change). Leave as is.
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 9 and 10; decision 0008 ('a missing or malformed key is a 500 configuration problem that names the key, never a silent default in code'; 'impossible calendar dates answer 400'); docs/17 section 3 (problem details); the file is readable with shell tools even though the Read tool is denied, so the fix can be made and tested without changing the deny rule; tests apps/api/src/test/api.test.ts; preview recordings of data-health.

- **source:** Seen in code; partly on record: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/08_CALCULATIONS_SPEC.md section 3 ('flag short_period so the UI can show NM per config (irr.minHoldYearsForDisplay)'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 12 (line 617 and 652: 'The five second wait ... stays in code'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/config/definitions.json.

Config keys nothing reads: aiBudgets (maxTokensPerDocument, maxDailySpendUsd, pauseOnBudget; extraction uses extraction.runTokenBudget and dayTokenBudget), irr.method, annualization, showGrossAndNet, dayCount and minHoldYearsForDisplay, dealTypes, platformStack, currencyStorage, priorYear, agentModels (named in page copy only).

Delete config keys nothing reads, or wire them (for example aiBudgets as the one AI budget block). Keep descriptive keys (priorYear, currencyStorage, platformStack) as Data Dictionary text and mark them as such (see EE-31).
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rules 7, 9 and 12; decision 0008 (strict config readers in apps/api/src/common/definitions.ts); docs/08 section 3 and fixtures for IRR; apps/worker-py/src/calc_check mirrors the 365-day rule; the web imports config/definitions.json directly in several page helpers (apps/web/src/pages/valuations/board.ts, monitoring/monitoring.ts, capital/notices.ts, pipeline/pipeline.ts, weekly/weekly.ts, intake/intake.ts, deal/data.ts), so the browser and the API can read the same key; SEC-8.9 (token budgets); Data Dictionary page shows every key.

### EE-31: The Data Dictionary page prints the whole of config/definitions.json to every signed-in role, raw keys, JSON blocks and internal notes included: is it a glossary for people or a settings dump, and who should read it?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/04_MODULES.md M1 ('Data Dictionary page that renders config/definitions.json'); seen in code against /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/06_BRAND_AND_UI.md section 6 ('A page never cites the build guide ... configuration key or file path').
- **builtDefault:** GET /api/v1/data-dictionary (roles [], any signed-in user) answers { source: 'config/definitions.json', definitions } (apps/api/src/data/data.controller.ts lines 80 to 83). apps/web/src/pages/Data.tsx (lines 115 to 170) prints 'Single source of truth: config/definitions.json', then a table of every top-level key as written (valuationVarianceFlag, unitSanityBoundsUsd) with its value; a nested block is shown as JSON text, including each block's _note, which cites documents, control ids and decision numbers ('docs/04 M16, docs/18 section 3, SEC-12', 'decision 0018'), and values such as 'Option B: custom Azure app' and 'convert in packages/calc/units.ts'. Only top-level keys that start with an underscore are hidden. Token budgets, cost rates and agent model names are shown to viewers.
- **alternatives:** Turn the page into a glossary: a config file of terms with a plain label, a plain definition and the current value formatted for people, and nothing else. Keep the raw dump but for platform admins only, on an admin route, and give other roles the glossary. Keep as built and strip _note keys and file names.
- **context:** docs/06 section 6 and apps/web/src/lib/copy-guard.test.ts (which reads page source, so it cannot see text that arrives from the API); docs/04 M1 and M5 ('diagnostic views available only to admins on an admin route'); CLAUDE.md rules 9, 15 and 16 (plain language); config/role_focus.json puts 'Data dictionary: Field definitions and display rules' in the platform admin work list; contract s.dataDictionary; preview recordings; tests apps/web Data page tests and apps/api/src/test/api.test.ts.

### EE-32: The deal team's 'route' of a change request and the approver's ticket approval are written through a system step because the table policies admit operations and the service actor only: should narrower column grants replace the system step?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 8 owner items (line 275: 'a narrower migration giving those roles the exact columns would remove the step').
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/common/system-step.ts switches the roles to the service role inside the caller's transaction while keeping the actor. apps/api/src/change-requests/change-requests.service.ts line 251 runs the write as a system step when the caller does not work the queue (the deal team routing); apps/api/src/capital/capital.service.ts lines 329, 336, 468 and 577 lock, read the register and write as a system step when the caller is not a writer (an approver approving a ticket; reads of the wire register for callers outside it); apps/api/src/capital/wires.service.ts line 496 and apps/api/src/pipeline/pipeline.service.ts line 561 use it for the sweep and the closing reopen. The machine and the API check the role before the step; the database does not.
- **alternatives:** A migration granting deal_team update on the routing columns of mon.deal_change_request and approver update on the approval columns of mon.trade_ticket and mon.capital_notice, with policies, so the database itself enforces who may write what (adds permissions to roles, which docs/05 and CLAUDE.md section 4 treat as a security role change to ask about). Keep the system step (no role gains a database permission; the API is the only guard). Keep the step and add a database trigger that checks the actor recorded on the row.
- **context:** SEC-5.1, SEC-5.6, SEC-12.3; CLAUDE.md rule 3 (least privilege) and the ask-before list (changing security roles); docs/05 checklist; migrations 0013 and 0014; packages/db/src/rls/matrix.ts write cells; decision 0013 (API as built and review fixes: a nested system step no longer drops the service role). Defence in depth argues for the database enforcing it; least privilege argues against wider grants; both are stated in the record.

### EE-33: The access choices the build made in migrations 0007 to 0013 were each left with 'say if any should be reverted' and 'review before any real deployment': are they confirmed?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 1 (line 39: decision 0004 and the RLS matrix 'both encode judgment calls about who sees what'), Session 4 (line 123), Session 5 close-out (line 170), Session 6 (line 202), Session 7 (line 236), Session 8 (lines 275 to 277).

Migrations 0014 to 0017 are recorded in decision 0013 as accepted or decided by the owner on 2026-11-11.

Narrow where a narrower rule works (for example aliases written by operations only; a platform admin reading the masked wire register is at odds with 'a platform admin gets no data access' in SEC-5.4 as decision 0013 reads it). Revert single items named by the owner.
- **context:** docs/05 (SEC-5.1 to SEC-5.6, SEC-12.2, the security role checklist), CLAUDE.md rules 3 and 5 and section 6 ('Security role impact reviewed'); decision 0004, 0009, 0010, 0012, 0013; packages/db/migrations 0006 to 0016; pnpm test:rls (1,775 cells). A question about walling history tables is recorded in decision 0023 (question 12) and belongs to another reader; decision 0013 notes history tables and the audit trail were not opened to the deal team.

### EE-34: The idempotency store is never purged, has no replay window, seals its fingerprint with the wire register's key, and the extraction batch route is not idempotent: what should each be?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 8 (line 280) and Session 9 (line 316: 'Still open from Session 8: the idempotency store's purge and key'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md ('Not changed, for the owner to decide').
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/common/idempotency.ts with table ops.idempotency_key (migration 0015): a key is reserved before the command, the 2xx answer is stored, a repeat replays it; rows are deleted only when a refused command releases its reservation (line 278). Nothing removes old rows; the classification registry marks the table operational, two years. request_hash is sealed with the field cipher keyed by PB_FIELD_KEY_HEX, the same key as bank details (apps/api/src/common/fieldCipher.ts). 26 routes are flagged idempotent in packages/contracts/src/routes*.ts; POST /api/v1/extraction/batches is not, although docs/17 says all POSTs that create financial records or trigger jobs accept the header. The web keeps a key for five minutes while an outcome is unknown (kept as built by the owner on 2026-11-11).
- **alternatives:** A purge job (a tested runbook in ops/runbooks, as CLAUDE.md 3c asks) with the window in config, and a replay window after which a repeated key runs as new or is refused. A separate key for the fingerprint (a second environment secret, to be held in Key Vault after merge). Flag the batch route idempotent. Keep as built for the prototype.
- **context:** docs/17 section 3 (idempotency as built); SEC-6.3 (keys), SEC-2 retention classes; CLAUDE.md rule 2 (secrets in Key Vault through managed identities), rule 8, and the ask-before list (deleting data: a purge deletes rows, though they are operational copies of answers and not business records); packages/contracts/src/idempotency.ts (shared rules), preview shim answers by the same rules; tests apps/api idempotency suite and the route parity test.

### EE-35: docs/17 says updates require If-Match, but the API treats the header as optional: should a command without it be refused?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 8 (line 280) and Session 9 (line 316); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md ('Judgment calls for the owner'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/17_ENGINEERING_STANDARDS.md section 3 ('updates require If-Match').
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/common/row-version.ts: without the header, or with '*', the command runs on the record as it stands; the row is locked and the write is guarded on the state and row version read under that lock either way; a stale version named in the header answers 412 stale-row-version; a malformed header is 400. Routes that read the header are marked ifMatch in the contract (valuations, valuation suggestions, capital notices, wire instructions, change requests, opportunities, closings, reporting packages, base cases and the two Draft corrections). The web app sends If-Match on those routes.
- **alternatives:** Require the header on every route marked ifMatch (428 Precondition Required or 400 when absent), so no client can overwrite what it has not read; keep '*' as the explicit way to say 'whatever is there'. Keep optional and change the sentence in docs/17 to match. Require it only on edits of figures (Draft valuation, Draft base case) and leave state commands optional.
- **context:** docs/17 section 3; packages/contracts/src/row-version.ts (one parser, conformance table run by contracts, API and preview); the preview shim must answer the same; tests: the row-version conformance tests, every API command test that omits the header, Playwright journeys, scripts/e2e-api.mjs; a future connector for Power Apps (decision 0019, another reader) would have to send the header.

### EE-36: Outside production the API encrypts bank details with a documented development key when no key is set: is that acceptable, and what replaces it?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 8 owner items (line 276: 'Key Vault wiring is production work'); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md (consequences).
- **builtDefault:** apps/api/src/common/fieldCipher.ts returns a FieldCipher over DEV_FIELD_KEY (packages/db/src/fieldCrypto.ts) when PB_FIELD_KEY_HEX is unset and the mode is not production; apps/api/src/composition.ts (around line 283) makes the production guard refuse to start without the key, naming 'prototype field key (PB_FIELD_KEY_HEX unset)'. The seed loader and the RLS fixture use the same development key (packages/db/src/seed.ts line 620, packages/db/src/rls/fixture.ts). The seed CLI refuses production without a key. All seeded bank details are synthetic.
- **alternatives:** Keep as built (a prototype with synthetic data and no secret store). Generate a random key per local database on first seed and keep it in a gitignored file, so no key is in the repository at all. Wire a KeyVault-shaped adapter with a mock now so production is configuration only (the real vault needs the employer's Azure, which only exists after merge).
- **context:** CLAUDE.md rule 2 and rule 13; SEC-6.3, SEC-12.2, SEC-17.6; docs/17 section 6 (production guard); decision 0001 (outside systems behind adapters); the idempotency store reuses this key (EE-34); gitleaks-style secret formats in scripts/check-employer-data.sh; tests apps/api composition and wires suites.

### EE-37: Two gaps in the seed that the Phase 3 rules exposed: the seeded wall has no operations member, and seeded Locked valuations have no approval rows. Should the seed be completed?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/SESSION_LOG.md Session 8 (line 281) and Session 9 (line 316); /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/decisions/0013-phase3-controls.md ('Judgment calls for the owner').
- **builtDefault:** tools/synthetic/src/generate.ts lines 977 to 989: the walled_deal wall has members deal.three and head.one. A walled change request can be routed but not started or applied, and a walled flagged row cannot be rejected (EE-3). Seeded Locked and Reopened valuations carry real lock hashes and prepared_by and approver columns but no rows in the valuation approval table, so the version screen names the signers from the row itself and the API answers an empty approvals list; valuations locked through the workflow do have approval rows.
- **alternatives:** Add ops.two to the seeded wall and seed approval rows for every seeded Locked valuation (deterministic times already exist: period end plus 40 days). Seed only the approval rows. Leave the seed and keep the page's fallback. Any seed change is made in streams of its own so nothing else moves, the way the base cases were added.
- **context:** docs/14 sections 1 and 3 (walled_deal scenario), docs/18 section 1; the seed 42 dataset is pinned by digest tests in tools/synthetic and by the preview recordings (apps/web/scripts/build-preview.mjs), the published demo and the site's media; mock users file with roles (apps/api mock identity); source reviewers and segregation-of-duties tests read approval rows; RLS matrix fixture is separate (packages/db/src/rls/fixture.ts) and already tests a wall with and without members.

### EE-38: When a deal closes, it becomes one position in the vehicle with the largest allocation, and its company is matched to the record by exact name only: is one position right when several vehicles invest, and should the name guards run when a company is created?

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/11_CAPABILITY_CHECKLIST.md row A8 ('Duplicate / near-miss name guard on new records: P (match guards stored and seeded; enforcement on record creation arrives with M1 linking services)'). The single-vehicle rule is seen in code (/home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/api/src/pipeline/convert.ts) and is not recorded as a choice.
- **builtDefault:** convertToInvestment in apps/api/src/pipeline/convert.ts: largestAllocation picks the line with the largest received (else requested) amount, then by vehicle name, and one core.investment is written for that vehicle only, with the next INV number, entry date from the closing, no cost and no cash flow (nothing invented). The other vehicles' allocation lines stay on the deal and get no position. The company: when the deal names no company on record, the canonical name is looked up exactly; a hit is reused, otherwise a new core.portfolio_company is inserted. No alias lookup, no near-miss check and no match guard check runs, so a deal whose company name is a near miss of an existing company, or on the guard list, silently creates a second company. Opportunity creation (apps/api/src/pipeline) runs no name check either.
- **alternatives:** One position per vehicle that received an allocation (each fund holds its own position; clients look through by ownership), with the allocation amounts as each position's commitment. Keep one position and record why. For the company: run the one linker every intake uses (linkInvestment and the company directory in packages/validation: exact name, alias, then near misses and guards as candidates) and, on a near miss or a guarded name, stop the conversion for a person to choose, or open a data exception and proceed with a new company.
- **context:** docs/04 M1 (match guards never auto-match; AC test), M5 AC ('converted into an Investment on close'), M8 ('commitment by vehicle'), M22; docs/03 section 5 (linking rules), core.company_alias, match guard table; decision 0004 (positions stored once, client views by look-through of vehicle positions); docs/18 section 4; SEC-5.3 (walls carried to the position: carryWalls); synthetic closings (docs/14 section 10: one vehicle-level line or one line per client); journey 6 (apps/web/e2e/journey6.spec.ts) asserts the converted position carries no figure; reporting, valuations and capital notices all key on core.investment, so more than one position per deal changes what operations mark each quarter.

- **source:** /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/docs/06_BRAND_AND_UI.md section 6 ('No document path, control number, module or phase number, configuration key or file path in anything a person reads'); the known fault /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/valuations/board.ts line 28. The guard /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/lib/copy-guard.test.ts catches only docs/NN, SEC-n, (Mn), 'Phase n' and '(config <lowercase letter>', so '(config/definitions.json)' passes.

(2) portfolio/Watchlist.tsx:164 and 167 heading and table label 'Thresholds (config/definitions.json)'; :169 an unlabelled threshold shows its key; :172 the config key is printed beside every value ('0.15 ebitdaYoYDeclinePct'); the aside 'Percentages are decimals (0.15 = 15%)'; :177 'Thresholds change in configuration, never in code'. (3) monitoring/monitoring.ts:30 thresholdsLine ends '(config/definitions.json documents)'. (4) pipeline/StageActions.tsx:166 'the checklist from config/closing_checklist.json opens with it'. (5) pipeline/NewOpportunity.tsx:281 '(config/diligence_templates.json)'. (6) pipeline/ClosingPanel.tsx:404 'From config/closing_checklist.json.' (7) lib/assistant.ts:114 MOCK_ASSISTANT_LINE '(config/definitions.json agentModels.default = mock)'. (8) pages/Data.tsx:133 'Single source of truth: config/definitions.json' (text from the API, apps/api/src/data/data.controller.ts:82) and the dictionary table itself (EE-31). (9) admin/Admin.tsx:362 'the AI kill switches (ai.*)'. (10) components/PreviewGuide.tsx:181 and 201 'turn ai.extraction on under Flags'. (11) The API sentence 'AI extraction is switched off (ai.extraction)' (apps/api/src/extraction/runner.ts:142, copied in apps/web/src/preview/routes/extraction.ts:250) is shown as written through plainRule (apps/web/src/pages/extraction/extraction.ts:413); lib/references.ts strips document, control and module references only. Where the reader may want to know a value can be changed, say who changes it ('set by a platform admin'), not where it is stored. Widen the guard: fail on 'config/', '.json', a dotted lower-case key in parentheses, 'pnpm', and flag keys; and strip a trailing '(key)' from API sentences in references.ts or stop the API writing it. Keep file names on pages that only a platform admin reads.
- **context:** docs/06 section 6; the owner's request of 2026-11-12 that the product not read as machine-made; CLAUDE.md rule 16 (plain language); tests that pin current wording and will need to follow: apps/web page tests beside each file (for example Watchlist, Valuations, monitoring tests), apps/api/src/test/extraction.test.ts (pins the refusal text), apps/web/src/preview/routes/extraction.test.ts, Playwright specs that read these strings, the preview probe (apps/web/scripts/probe-preview.mjs); labels for flags could come from the flag's own description column (ops.feature_flag has one, seeded in migrations 0003, 0010, 0011, 0023). Sample file names in the preview guide (weekly-values-defects.csv, ops-drop-clean.xlsx) are names a person picks from a list and are a different case.

### EE-41: Three forms ask a person to type a document id as a UUID: should they offer a picker of the documents the person can see?

- **source:** Seen in code: /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/pipeline/DiligenceTasks.tsx lines 75 to 82 ('Evidence document id', 'A document id is a UUID as the document hub shows it.'), /home/user/Portfolio-Beach/apps/web/src/pages/pipeline/ClosingPanel.tsx lines 239 and 737. Not recorded as open.

The base case form (apps/web/src/pages/deal/UnderwritingForm.tsx around lines 226 and 512) already offers a list of the position's documents by title.

Let the system propose the document itself when exactly one classified document of the expected type exists for the deal, for the person to confirm. Keep the typed id.
